### [246] [History-Conditioned Flow Matching for Probabilistic Dynamics of Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25658)
*Hang Yang,Tingcong Liu,Junjie Xiong,Fangju Yang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种历史条件化、物理信息驱动的流匹配框架，用于腱驱动连续体机器人的概率动力学预测，通过运动与驱动历史预测下一完整关节构型分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动连续体机器人的确定性动力学建模面临材料行为、腱传动、摩擦和接触等不确定性；实测关节构型与名义腱指令无法完全刻画内部力学因素，导致后续运动预测存在不确定性。

Method: 采用历史条件化的物理信息流匹配（flow-matching）框架进行概率动力学预测，利用运动与驱动历史预测下一完整关节构型的分布；在给定指令下递归采样下一步构型，从而预测未来整体运动的分布。

Result: 仿真中，场景专用模型在内部摩擦变化下五秒轨迹Energy Score为12.05 mm，在未观测驱动扰动下为9.29 mm；相比条件变分自编码器与扩散基线，Flow获得更低Energy Score和更接近名义水平的覆盖率；消融实验支持历史与结构条件化的作用。物理机器人上，在两个未参与训练的腱指令曲线下，五秒Energy Score为11.91和11.42 mm，低于对比基线；预测与实测离散度比值为1.65和1.22（越接近1越好）。

Conclusion: 结果支持在机械观测不完整条件下进行历史条件化的概率动力学预测。

Abstract: Deterministic dynamics modeling of tendon-driven continuum robots remains challenging owing to uncertainties in material behavior, tendon transmission, friction, and contact. Measured joint configurations and nominal tendon commands do not fully characterize these internal mechanical factors, leaving uncertainty in the subsequent motion. We therefore develop a history-conditioned, physics-informed flow-matching framework for probabilistic dynamics prediction, using motion and actuation histories to predict the distribution of the next complete joint configuration. By recursively sampling next-step configurations under prescribed commands, the model predicts distributions of future whole-body motions. In simulation, scenario-specific models achieve five-second trajectory Energy Scores (lower is better) of 12.05 mm under internal friction variation and 9.29 mm under unobserved actuation disturbances. Relative to the conditional variational autoencoder and diffusion baselines, Flow attains lower Energy Scores and coverage closer to the nominal level in both scenarios. Ablations support history and structural conditioning in both scenarios. On the physical robot, predictions under two tendon-command profiles excluded from training capture the principal motion sequences, with five-second Energy Scores of 11.91 and 11.42 mm, lower than the compared baselines. The predicted-to-measured spread ratios are 1.65 and 1.22 (closer to 1 is better). These results support history-conditioned probabilistic dynamics prediction under incomplete mechanical observations.

</details>


### [247] [Deploying Foundation Models for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25666)
*Vishnu Sashank Dorbala,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对基础模型在具身导航部署中的训练偏见与上下文长度限制两个问题，提出TAP与MemCtrl两种方法，并在真实环境与多种任务上验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署基础模型于具身导航智能体面临两大挑战：一是基础模型的训练偏见导致在未见环境中个性化表现差；二是基础模型上下文长度有限，尤其在长时程任务中影响成功率。

Method: 首先构建了基于基础模型的具身导航文献分类体系并指出上述局限。针对训练偏见问题提出Transit-Aware Planning (TAP)，通过从场景中挖掘人类习惯数据对基础模型进行预置；针对上下文限制提出MemCtrl，通过新颖的"记忆头"增强实现主动记忆管理。

Result: TAP在实验室真实环境中使用Turtlebot进行个性化目标查找，相比非TAP基线平均提升18%；MemCtrl在多种具身任务上平均提升6%，在长指令子集上提升20%，且仅使用基线模型约一半的上下文。

Conclusion: 基于上述结果，作者阐述了其关于基础模型具身智能体在真实环境可部署性的立场，并指出了未来开放研究方向。

Abstract: We present and tackle two problems associated with deploying Foundation Models (FMs) on Embodied Agents performing navigation: 1) Training bias in FMs leading to poor personalization in unseen environments, and 2) Limited FM context length hindering success, especially on long horizon tasks. Our solution for the former involves priming the FM with human-habit data mined from the scene and our solution for the latter involves active memory management via a novel `memory head' augmentation. We first present a taxonomy of existing literature on FM-based Embodied Navigation, and highlight these limitations. We then present our approaches, Transit-Aware Planning (TAP) and MemCtrl to address the limitations. With TAP, we present real-world results in a lab environment with a Turtlebot for personalized target finding that shows an average improvement of 18% over a non-TAP baseline. On MemCtrl, we report a 6% average improvement across various embodied tasks, with 20% on long instruction subsets, all while using nearly half the context used in the baseline model. Motivated by these result, we present our stance the deployability of FM-based embodied agents in real-world environments, and highlight open research directions.

</details>


### [248] [CDKF-Track: Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering for Cooperative 3D Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25668)
*Maria Damanaki,Nikos Piperigkos,Alexandros Gkillas,Aris S. Lalos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CDKF-Track，一种面向协同3D多目标跟踪的聚类感知数据驱动卡尔曼滤波框架，联合解决检测级融合冗余与可学习运动建模问题，在V2V4Real数据集上跟踪精度最高提升27.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单智能体多目标跟踪受遮挡、传感器噪声和场景理解不完整影响；多智能体系统虽提升鲁棒性，但引入冗余测量导致错误数据关联，且难以刻画非线性目标运动。

Method: 首先基于图拉普拉斯公式融合多车3D LiDAR检测；其次用聚类感知冗余削减方案对空间相关检测分组并选取代表性观测；随后用数据驱动卡尔曼滤波器学习运动动态；最后用小波时域细化模块抑制短期位置波动。

Result: 在真实V2V4Real数据集上，相比现有多智能体MOT方法，跟踪精度最高提升27.99%。

Conclusion: CDKF-Track是首个在协同3D MOT中同时处理检测级融合冗余与可学习运动建模的框架。

Abstract: Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) is essential for EdgeAI perception systems, where accurate object localization and reliable identification enable safe decision-making. Singleagent MOT suffers from occlusions, sensor noise, and partial scene understanding in complex real-world scenarios. While multi-agent systems improve robustness by exploiting shared information, they introduce redundant measurements that lead to false data associations, and still struggle to capture nonlinear object dynamics. To address these challenges, we propose CDKFTrack, a Cluster-aware Data-Driven Kalman Filtering framework for Cooperative 3D MOT. The proposed method first fuses multivehicle 3D LiDAR detections through a Graph Laplacian-based formulation. Then, a cluster-aware redundancy reduction scheme groups spatially related detections and selects representative observations to reduce duplicate inputs to the tracker. The resulting detections are processed by a data-driven Kalman filter that learns object motion dynamics from data, reducing dependence on predefined linear motion assumptions. Furthermore, a wavelet-based temporal refinement module leverages the multiresolution decomposition property of wavelets to attenuate shortterm positional fluctuations and improve trajectory continuity. To the best of our knowledge, CDKF-Track is the first framework to jointly address detection-level fusion redundancy and learnable motion modeling in cooperative 3D MOT. Experimental results on the real-world V2V4Real dataset indicate that CDKF-Track achieves up to 27.99% improvements in tracking accuracy over state-of-the-art multi-agent MOT methods.

</details>


### [249] [Teaching Reinforcement Learning and Humanoid Robotics to High-School Students: An Expert-Validated Curriculum Design on a Low-Cost Open Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25674)
*Yuanzhe Dong,Jie Cao,Shuman Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个以共享机器人实物为核心的整合式机器人课程框架，通过并行学科主线、依赖排序、仿真与硬件渐进融合、分层考核点及协作与个人责任平衡设计，将完整科研流程转化为适合高中生的课程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本开源机器人与强化学习仿真工具为中小学生接触前沿机器人技术创造了机会，但把涵盖机械装配、电路搭建、仿真、策略学习、系统辨识和实物部署的完整科研流程转化为适合初学者的连贯课程仍很困难。

Method: 采用迭代设计过程，围绕开源人形机器人项目构建高中课程：学生两两合作完成装配、在仿真中训练行走策略并部署到实物平台。框架包含并行学科主线、基于技术依赖的排序、仿真与硬件渐进整合、分层表现检查点，以及兼顾团队协作与个人问责的结构，并经过五位机器人研究、工程、中学STEM教育与课程设计专家的形成性评审。

Result: 专家反馈揭示三大核心设计张力：真实性与认知负荷、系统集成与及时可见进展、团队构建与个人问责；这些张力被纳入最终框架。

Conclusion: 本研究为将机器人科研工作流改编为跨学科中小学课程提供了结构化方法，未来需开展课堂研究以检验实施效果与学生学习情况。

Abstract: Lower cost open source robots and reinforcement learning (RL) simulation tools create new opportunities for precollege students to engage with contemporary robotics. However, translating a complete research workflow, spanning mechanical assembly, electrical setup, simulation, policy learning, system identification, and physical deployment, into a coherent course for novice learners remains challenging. We present an integrated robotics course framework that organizes these activities around a shared robotic artifact. The framework combines parallel disciplinary tracks, sequencing based on technical dependencies, progressive integration of simulation and hardware, layered performance checkpoints, and structures for balancing collaborative work with individual accountability. We illustrate the framework through a high school curriculum organized around a robot project in which pairs of students assemble an open source humanoid robot, train a walking policy in simulation, and deploy it on the physical platform. The framework was developed through an iterative design process that included formative review by five experts in robotics research, engineering, secondary STEM education, and curriculum design. Expert feedback highlighted three central design tensions: authenticity versus cognitive load, system integration versus timely visible progress, and team construction versus individual accountability. These tensions informed the final framework presented in this paper. This work offers a structured approach for adapting robotics research workflows into interdisciplinary precollege courses; future classroom studies are needed to examine implementation and student learning.

</details>


### [250] [SG-CPG: Severity-Gated Central Pattern Generators for Adaptive Quadruped Locomotion under Continuous Actuator Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25687)
*Adarsh Kumar Kosta,Kaushik Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受动物卸载患肢并随损伤程度渐进调整步态的启发，作者提出SG-CPG，在冻结健康CPG策略的基础上引入残差门与幅度门，使四足机器人在执行器渐进退化下仍能维持运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物在肢体力量部分丧失时并不必然切换步态，而是卸载患肢、重新协调其余肢体并按损伤严重度调整反应，从而在部分失能下继续运动，而非在"健康"与"失效"之间离散切换。作者希望把这种渐进式适应引入四足机器人，以应对执行器的连续退化。

Method: 提出SG-CPG：保留一个冻结的健康CPG策略，加入两个由严重度驱动的门控——残差门（对所有四条腿重新协调）与幅度门（随退化加剧逐步缩短患肢步幅）。用两种机制模拟渐进退化：降低关节力矩上限（ceiling机制）与缩放底层控制器增益（gain机制）。

Result: Unitree Go2仿真中，在关节力量损失95%的条件下，SG-CPG在全方位指令序列下保持trot步态、存活率100%，指令跟踪误差在8%以内。在降低力矩上限时，去掉任一条严重度路径（残差的严重度观测或幅度门），会使患关节的力矩削波从4.4%升至80%损失下的13.6%和26.3%步数占比。真机Go2上，在高达93%小腿力矩退化下，SG-CPG在29次前进与转向试验中成功28次。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于严重度门控的适应机制可将健康运动策略扩展到执行器的渐进退化场景，而无需将故障视为离散失效。

Abstract: An animal with a weakened limb does not necessarily switch its gait, instead it unloads the affected limb, re-coordinates the remaining limbs, and scales its response with injury severity. This graded adaptation allows locomotion to persist despite partial loss of limb strength, rather than requiring a discrete transition between healthy and failed. Inspired by this behavior, we propose SG-CPG, a central pattern generator (CPG) for quadruped locomotion under continuous actuator degradation. SG-CPG preserves a frozen healthy CPG policy and introduces two severity-driven gates: a residual gate that re-coordinates all four legs and an amplitude gate that progressively shortens the weakened leg's stride as degradation increases. We emulate progressive degradation through two mechanisms: lowering the joint torque ceiling (ceiling mechanism) and scaling its low-level controller gains (gain mechanism), representing distinct forms of actuator weakening. Our simulations on a Unitree Go2 show that SG-CPG maintains a trot gait with 100% survival across an omnidirectional command schedule under 95% joint strength loss while tracking commands within 8%. Under a lowered torque ceiling, removing either severity path, the residual's severity observation or the amplitude gate, raises clipping at the weakened joint from 4.4% to 13.6% and 26.3% of steps at an 80% loss. On a real Go2, SG-CPG survives 28 of 29 forward and turning trials with up to 93% calf torque degradation. These results show that severity-gated adaptation can extend a healthy locomotion policy to progressive actuator degradation without treating the fault as a discrete failure.

</details>


### [251] [MatcherCompass: A Deployment-Aware Benchmark to Guide Image Matcher Selection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25688)
*Hyunwoo Kim,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MatcherCompass，一个面向现场机器人的部署感知局部特征匹配器基准，在统一输入与位姿评估下比较九种匹配流程，跨四种分辨率、数值精度、四种视觉条件及四款 GPU 平台，测量精度、运行时、显存与能耗，并整理出满足用户时间与资源约束的选型指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场机器人需在不同时段与感知模态下运行，要求在其板载时间与资源预算内获得准确的图像对应关系。然而，单个方法在单一设备上报告的精度与运行时间难以为目标平台上的匹配器及其配置选择提供有效指导，缺乏部署感知的选型依据。

Method: 在统一输入与位姿评估流程下，构建部署感知基准 MatcherCompass，比较九种经典与学习型匹配流程，覆盖四种图像分辨率与支持的数值精度。视觉条件包括视角变化、可见光与热红外的昼夜匹配以及白天可见光—热红外匹配。位姿精度用 5°、10°、20° 下的误差—召回曲线下面积（AUC）评估，并在四款涵盖工作站与板载计算机的 GPU 平台上测量每对图像的运行时、显存与能耗。

Result: 结果显示，硬件、输入分辨率与数值精度的变化会使匹配器跨越运行时预算边界，从而改变可行选择。测量结果被整理为选型指南，可返回满足用户指定时间与资源限制的所有配置，并给出其在所选视觉条件下的精度。

Conclusion: MatcherCompass 为选择适配机器人感知条件与计算硬件的匹配流程提供了实测依据。

Abstract: Field robots operating across time of day and sensing modalities require accurate image correspondences within onboard time and resource budgets. However, accuracy and runtime reported for individual methods on a single device provide limited guidance for choosing a matcher and its configuration on a target platform. We present MatcherCompass, a deployment-aware benchmark for choosing local feature matchers in field robotics. Under common input and pose-evaluation procedures, we compare nine classical and learned matching pipelines across four image resolutions and supported numerical precisions. Four visual conditions cover viewpoint variation, day--night matching in visible and thermal imagery, and daytime visible--thermal matching. We evaluate pose accuracy using the area under the error--recall curve (AUC) at $5^\circ$, $10^\circ$, and $20^\circ$, and measure runtime, GPU memory, and energy per image pair on four GPU platforms spanning workstation and onboard computers. The results show that changes in hardware, input resolution, and numerical precision can move a matcher across a runtime budget boundary, altering the feasible choices. We organize the measurements into a selection guide that returns all configurations satisfying user-specified time and resource limits, together with their accuracy under the selected visual condition. MatcherCompass provides measured evidence for choosing matching pipelines that fit a robot's sensing conditions and computing hardware.
  Project page: https://matchercompass.github.io/.

</details>


### [252] [MotionForge: A Data Generation Pipeline and Large-Scale Benchmark for Long-Horizon Manipulation of Dynamic Objects with Domain Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25689)
*Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Haotian Xian,Ruyang Han,Jiayi Sun,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Luxi Li,Kaimin Mao,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 MotionForge，首个面向动态操作的大规模仿真基准与数据生成流程，可联合评估域偏移与长时程交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型机器人策略多在静态或准静态环境中评估，而动态操作中物体与场景持续演化；当前动态仿真基准又多聚焦短时程、反应式交互，运动模式简单，且缺乏对域偏移下系统评估与模型无关实时执行协议的支持。

Method: MotionForge 包含 40 个动态交互任务，覆盖 11 种运动模式，其中 17 个为长时程任务；引入两项关键设计：(1) 系统评估协议，考察单因素域偏移与联合域偏移下的策略鲁棒性；(2) 解耦的、延迟感知的执行协议，使环境演化独立于策略推理时间。

Result: 对代表性通用机器人策略的广泛评估显示，它们在联合域偏移下存在显著局限，暴露出当前策略能力与动态物体长时程鲁棒操作需求之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: MotionForge 可作为具身智能未来研究的综合测试平台，推动动态操作下域偏移与长时程交互的鲁棒策略研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in learning-based robot policies have demonstrated promising progress, yet they are predom- inantly evaluated in static or quasi-static environments. In dynamic manipulation, objects and scenes continuously evolve while the robot perceives, reasons, and acts. However, recent dynamic simulation benchmarks largely focus on short-horizon, reactive interactions with simple motion patterns and offer limited support for both systematic evaluation under domain shifts and model-agnostic real-time execution protocols. To bridge these gaps, we introduce MotionForge, the first large- scale simulation benchmark and data-generation pipeline tailored to jointly evaluate domain shifts and long-horizon interaction in dynamic manipulation. MotionForge comprises 40 dynamic interaction tasks spanning 11 distinct motion patterns, with dedicated support for 17 long-horizon tasks. Our benchmark introduces two key novelties: (1) a systematic evaluation protocol for assessing policy robustness under both single-factor (e.g., only backgrounds shift) and joint domain shifts (e.g., simultaneous shifts of objects, backgrounds, lighting, and speed); and (2) a decoupled, latency-aware execution protocol where the environ- ment continuously evolves independently of policy inference time. Extensive evaluations of representative general-purpose robot policies on our benchmark reveal substantial limitations under joint domain shifts. These findings expose a critical gap between current policy capabilities and the requirements of robust long- horizon manipulation of dynamic objects under domain shifts, establishing MotionForge as a comprehensive testbed for future research in embodied AI.

</details>


### [253] [Induced Riemannian Metrics for Motion Planning with Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25695)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Thomas Cohn,Miguel Angel Rogel Garcia,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文证明约束子流形上的诱导黎曼度量在隐式与显式表示下是等价的，据此将黎曼度量下的运动规划从无约束空间扩展到约束子流形，并在双臂Franka任务中对比了欧氏与动能度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束运动规划中，任务与闭环约束使机器人运动限于构型空间的低维弯曲子流形。现有方法中，隐式表示（约束水平集）常与构型空间的欧氏度量结合，显式表示（参数化）则与参数域度量结合，导致规划器所最小化的路径长度依赖于所选表示，表示与度量被不合理地耦合在一起。

Method: 作者从构型空间的黎曼度量出发，利用子流形继承的诱导度量来衡量路径长度。他们证明隐式与显式表示给出相同的诱导度量（仅坐标不同），因而几何相同，且该结论对任意黎曼度量成立，不仅限于欧氏度量。在此基础上，将黎曼度量下的规划从无约束空间推广到约束子流形，在采样规划器与轨迹优化器中均采用诱导度量；对显式表示，诱导度量还计入了参数化引入的形变。

Result: 在双臂Franka机械臂、带有末端执行器任务约束的双臂操作平台上进行实验，对比了欧氏度量与动能度量。结果显示诱导度量在两种表示下产生一致的几何与路径长度。

Conclusion: 度量选择与表示选择相互独立：无论采用隐式还是显式表示，诱导度量都给出相同的几何。该结论为在约束子流形上使用一般黎曼度量（如动能度量）提供了理论依据，使规划器能够一致地优化路径长度。

Abstract: In constrained motion planning problems, task and loop-closure constraints restrict a robot's motion to a curved, lower-dimensional submanifold of its configuration space. Planners measure path length with a metric, which sets the cost of moving in each direction. Under the Euclidean metric, this cost is the same everywhere, whereas under a general Riemannian metric, such as the kinetic-energy metric, the cost can vary with direction and configuration. Existing methods often describe the submanifold either implicitly, as a constraint level set, or explicitly, through a parameterization. The implicit representation is typically combined with the Euclidean metric of the configuration space, and the explicit representation with the parameter domain, so the path length that a planner minimizes depends on the representation. Instead, we measure path length with the induced metric, which the submanifold inherits from a Riemannian metric on the configuration space. The implicit and explicit representations yield the same induced metric, expressed in different coordinates, and hence the same geometry. This result holds for any Riemannian metric on the configuration space, not only the Euclidean one. The choice of metric is therefore independent of the choice of representation. Using this result, we extend planning under a Riemannian metric from unconstrained spaces to constraint submanifolds by applying the induced metric in both a sampling-based planner and a trajectory optimizer. For an explicit representation, the induced metric also accounts for the distortion that the parameterization introduces. In experiments on a bimanual manipulation setup with two Franka arms under end-effector task constraints, we compare the Euclidean and kinetic-energy metrics.

</details>


### [254] [The Cartesian Hand: In-Hand Manipulation with All-Linear Fingers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25696)
*Boxi Xia,Bokuan Li,Ryan Shin,Zijiang Yang,Jiaxun Liu,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出7自由度末端执行器Cartesian Hand，仅用直线运动在单一执行器内实现独立抓取与相对操作，在35个物体上演示多种灵巧操作任务，并可迁移至人形机器人双臂操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手具备丰富操作能力但结构与控制复杂，平行夹爪简单鲁棒却难以在抓取后操作物体；操作螺纹容器、制造工具、实验仪器等关节型物体常需第二个夹爪、外部夹具或手臂协同动作。

Method: 设计7自由度末端执行器：两个独立驱动的平行夹爪分别夹持物体不同部位，四个可平移指腹产生被夹持部件间的相对运动；指尖运动学与构型无关，操作可由简单直线运动基元组合而成，适用于螺纹、转轴、直线导轨、柱塞和扳机等机构。

Result: 在实验室、制造和家庭场景的35个物体上演示了开合瓶盖、移液、泵送、双把手操作、拧螺丝、扳机驱动和抓取内重定向；相同操作流程可从固定基座机械臂迁移至人形机器人，用两只Cartesian Hand完成双臂实验室操作。

Conclusion: 当独立抓取与相对运动被直接设计进末端执行器时，机械上简单的架构也能产生多样化的手内操作能力；将开源全部软硬件设计。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation has increasingly pursued human-like dexterous hands with many articulated degrees of freedom, offering rich manipulation capabilities at the cost of mechanical and control complexity. At the other extreme, parallel grippers are simple and robust, but provide little ability to manipulate an object after grasping it. Operating articulated objects such as threaded containers, manufacturing tools, and laboratory instruments often requires a second gripper, an external fixture, or coordinated arm motion. We introduce the Cartesian Hand, a 7-DoF end-effector that rethinks dexterous manipulation by combining independent grasping and relative manipulation within a single end-effector using only linear motion. Two independently actuated parallel grippers hold different parts of an object, while four translating fingertips generate relative motion between the grasped parts. Its configuration-independent fingertip kinematics allow manipulation to be composed from simple linear motion primitives. The Cartesian Hand is particularly suited to objects structured around common mechanisms such as threads, pivots, linear guides, plungers, and triggers. We demonstrate cap opening and closing, pipetting, pumping, two-handle manipulation, screwdriving, trigger actuation, and in-grasp reorientation across 35 objects spanning laboratory, manufacturing, and household settings. The same manipulation procedures transfer from a fixed-base robot arm to a humanoid, where we demonstrate bimanual laboratory manipulation using two Cartesian Hands. These results show that versatile in-hand manipulation capability can emerge from a mechanically simple architecture when independent grasping and relative motion are designed directly into the end-effector. We will open-source all software and hardware design. Our website is https://generalroboticslab.com/cartesian_handv1.

</details>


### [255] [Zephyron: Integrated Design and Analytical Evaluation of a Solar-Assisted Mobile Manipulator for Multimodal Environmental Reconnaissance and Distributed Visual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25709)
*Sabik Bin Sultan,Shafi Bin Sultan,Safwan Sadad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Zephyron四轮漫游车的文献驱动工程设计基线，用显式组件与几何参数替代无依据的数值假设，并给出可复现的设计与评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境侦察需要能在有限能量与通信条件下携带传感器、保留测量情境并返回可解释证据的移动平台，而现有原型设计缺乏有依据的数值与组件基线。

Method: 开展可复现文献检索（获5,000条记录、4,858条去重）并针对性审查原始文献与厂商资料；确定组件与几何基线（165 mm车轮、12 kg质量预算、72 Wh电池能量、20 W光伏模块）；进行力学与能量分析（滚阻系数0.04、10度坡、每轮约0.517 N·m；40 W运动负载下可用57.6 Wh续航1.44 h，25%驱动占空比无太阳能时4.19 h）；建立传感器模型与质量感知的停采样策略；构建轻量检测器、参考式传感器学习和可执行数据完整性检查的机器学习评估路径。

Result: 给出了明确的组件与几何基线及可计算场景（如爬坡扭矩、续航时长），说明积分时间、标定、温度和通信延迟如何约束传感器解读；输出可编辑3D模型、子系统图和可复现分析数据；所报告续航等均为计算场景而非实测性能。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一个可追溯的设计与评估框架，但需实验验证后才能赋予载荷、续航、检测或野外运行等级等性能指标。

Abstract: Environmental reconnaissance needs mobile platforms that carry sensors, preserve measurement context, and return interpretable evidence under limited energy and communication. We present a literature-informed engineering design for Zephyron, a four-wheel rover with a front manipulator, environmental sensors, distributed computer vision, local recording, and a raised rear solar module. The design keeps the prototype layout but replaces unsupported numerical assumptions with an explicit component and geometry baseline. A reproducible search retrieved 5,000 records (4,858 unique) for screening, followed by targeted review of primary literature and manufacturer documentation. The baseline uses 165 mm wheels, a 12 kg mass budget, a 72 Wh battery-energy basis, and a 20 W photovoltaic module. With rolling-resistance coefficient 0.04, steady ascent of a 10 degree grade needs about 0.517 N m per wheel under equal load sharing. An illustrative 40 W motion load gives 1.44 h from 57.6 Wh usable energy, and a 25 percent driving duty gives 4.19 h without solar input; these are calculated scenarios, not measured performance. Sensor models show how integration time, calibration, temperature, and communication delay constrain interpretation, and a quality-aware stop-and-sample policy links these constraints to mission execution. Lightweight detectors, reference-based sensor learning, and executable data-integrity checks define a reproducible machine-learning evaluation pathway. The contribution is a traceable design and evaluation framework with editable 3D models, subsystem diagrams, and reproducible analytical data. Experimental validation is required before assigning payload, endurance, detection, or field-operating ratings.

</details>


### [256] [Designing an Efficient Excavator Bucket for Lunar ISRU: A Comparative Study with Vision-Based Fill and Displacement Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25724)
*Abdulla Hil Kafi,Tomoki Koshi,Jorge Casir,Kenji Nagaoka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种螺旋腔式月球风化层挖掘轮，并配套"传感器-光照"评估栈，联合估计填充率、沉陷量与比能耗；台架实验显示其挖掘速率与能效优于文献基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球风化层挖掘设备通常依赖较重的多执行器机构，在质量、复杂度和能效敏感的任务中不利；因此需要更轻简、高效且可稳定评估性能的挖掘方案。

Method: 设计螺旋腔式挖掘轮；构建"传感器-光照"评估栈，利用视觉估计填充率与沉陷量，并由执行器日志计算比能耗；开展台架实验（四圈，5/10/15 RPM），与两种文献基准对比；提出保留指数η并与填充率关联；建立DEM仿真复现实验趋势。

Result: 相比桶鼓基准，挖掘质量与填充率更高，挖掘速率提高2.2–3.0倍，比能耗降低29%；归一化沉陷量（mm/kg）更低，表明牵引稳定、无下陷打滑；力矩包络平稳，切-运-卸循环在不同转速下可重复；保留指数η与填充率相关；DEM仿真以较低误差复现实验趋势。

Conclusion: 螺旋腔式挖掘轮有望在质量、简化和能效为任务驱动因素时替代更重的多执行器挖掘装置，但结论主要基于台架实验与DEM验证，尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper present a spiral-cavity wheel for lunar regolith excavation and a sensor-light evaluation stack that jointly estimates fill ratio (vision), sinkage (vision), and specific energy from actuator logs. In benchtop tests (four revolutions at 5, 10, and 15~RPM) against two literature baselines, the proposed wheel achieved higher excavated mass and fill ratio, delivering 2.2-3.0 times higher excavation rate while reducing specific energy by 29 % relative to a bucket-drum baseline. Normalized sinkage (mm/kg) was also lower, indicating stable traction without bogging. Effort-time traces show a steady torque envelope with repeatable cut-carry-dump cycles across speeds. We provide a retention index $η$ that correlates with fill ratio and a DEM setup that reproduces experimental trends with low error. Results suggest spiral-cavity wheels can replace heavier multi-actuator diggers when mass, simplicity, and energy efficiency are mission drivers.

</details>


### [257] [AgriGen: Large-Scale Scene Generation Framework for Photorealistic Agricultural Robotics Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25725)
*Utkarsh Bajpai,Serge Tleiji,Cédric Pradalier,Stéphanie Aravecchia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Isaac Sim、集成ROS的农业环境大规模程序化生成框架，用于支持农业机器人的仿真训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人研究受限于田间准入困难、田间条件不可控、地理差异和季节性作物周期，导致农业数据集获取困难且昂贵，评估受限、系统鲁棒性不足。其他机器人领域已通过高保真仿真实现学习与评估的规模化，但农业机器人仍缺乏同等能力的工具。

Method: 构建一个ROS集成、基于Isaac Sim的框架，实现农业环境的大规模程序化生成，支持照片级真实渲染、物理仿真和领域随机化，并内置对行栽作物、果园和葡萄园的支持，且可便捷扩展至其他作物类别。

Result: 该框架可在机器人研究相关规模上支持照片级真实渲染、物理仿真与领域随机化，并内置支持行栽作物、果园和葡萄园，可扩展至其他作物类型。

Conclusion: 该工作为农业机器人研究提供了可规模化的高保真仿真工具，有望缓解数据获取难题并提升系统评估与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Agricultural robotics is advancing rapidly, yet progress remains constrained by limited field access, lack of control over field conditions, geographic variability, and seasonal crop cycles. These factors make it difficult and costly to acquire diverse agricultural datasets, resulting in limited evaluation and reduced system robustness. While other robotics domains have scaled learning and evaluation through high-fidelity simulation, agricultural robotics still lacks comparably capable tools. In this paper, we present a ROS-integrated framework, built on Isaac Sim, for large-scale procedural generation of agricultural environments. The framework supports photorealistic rendering, physics simulation, and domain randomization at scales relevant to robotics research, with built-in support for row crops, orchards, and vineyards and straightforward extensibility to additional crop categories. Project Page: https://baj31415.github.io/agrigen/

</details>


### [258] [Dual Covariance Gaussian Splatting SLAM: Decoupling Rendering and Registration for Robust Real-Time Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25746)
*Edward Beng Wai Tan,Siew-Kei Lam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出双协方差参数化方法，为每个3D高斯同时维护渲染协方差与跟踪协方差，缓解渲染与配准的冲突，实现鲁棒的~60 FPS 3DGS SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于ICP的3DGS SLAM中，同一个高斯的协方差需同时服务于两个目标：映射器为最小化光度误差会将其压扁贴合表面，而稳健的配准通常更依赖测量不确定度。两种需求相互冲突。

Method: 提出双协方差参数化：每个高斯保留单一均值，但拥有两个协方差——由映射器优化的渲染协方差，以及由RGB-D传感器噪声模型导出的跟踪协方差。此外，将跟踪协方差用作图像角点的高斯锚点，在深度几何较弱的方向上提供约束。

Result: 在TUM RGB-D、ScanNet、Replica以及两段用RealSense D435i在轮式和手持平台上采集的户外序列上进行评估，在多个场景中实现鲁棒跟踪并降低里程计漂移，同时保持约60 FPS的跟踪速度。

Conclusion: 通过将渲染与跟踪的协方差解耦，并以传感器噪声模型引导跟踪协方差及角点锚点约束，可在不牺牲渲染需求的前提下提升3DGS SLAM的跟踪鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: ICP-based 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) SLAM tracks in real time by registering incoming frames against map Gaussians, using each primitive's covariance for both rendering and registration. These two uses place conflicting demands on one covariance. The mapper shapes it to minimize photometric error, often flattening it against surfaces, while robust registration typically benefits from measurement uncertainty. We propose a dual-covariance parameterization. Each Gaussian keeps a single mean but holds two covariances: a rendering covariance optimized by the mapper, and a tracking covariance derived from an RGB-D sensor noise model. We further use the tracking covariances as Gaussian anchors for image corners, providing constraints in directions where depth geometry is weak. We evaluate on TUM RGB-D, ScanNet, Replica, and two outdoor sequences recorded with a RealSense D435i on wheeled and handheld platforms. We achieve robust tracking performance across multiple scenes and reduced odometry drift, while tracking at $\sim$ 60 FPS.

</details>


### [259] [Fisheye-VLA: Decoupling Coverage and Acuity for Manipulation with a Single Fisheye Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25750)
*Ziang Ren,Zike Yan,Raymond Zhang,Xuguo He,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Fisheye-VLA，用单个被动鱼眼相机同时提供全局场景感知与局部细节反馈，替代传统的前置相机加腕部相机方案。通过受控重渲染研究确定局部视野应指向末端执行器，并据此设计紧凑的双手视野分配，配合射线编码与预训练 VLA，在扩展桌面区域分别取得 84% 和 82% 成功率，并支持货架与传送带操作，无需物理腕部相机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作任务既需要广域场景感知，也需要局部细节反馈，但传统相机支架通常通过独立的前置相机和腕部相机分别实现，硬件复杂且依赖额外相机。作者希望用单个被动鱼眼相机统一这两种能力，并解决"局部视觉预算应分配到哪里"这一关键设计问题。

Method: 提出 Fisheye-VLA 视觉接口：全局鱼眼视图保留整个工作空间，局部透视裁剪指向交互细节。通过受控重渲染研究，在相同录制的观测数据上比较不同裁剪方向，发现以末端执行器为中心的视图能捕获大部分候选池的估计收益，从而确定围绕双手的紧凑视野分配。界面使用标定的末端执行器投影与运动前导来跟踪裁剪区域，并用共享射线编码在裁剪移动时保持其空间含义。整体与预训练 VLA 集成。

Result: 在两个扩展桌面区域中分别达到 84% 和 82% 的成功率，其中部分目标位置超出前置相机覆盖范围；同时支持货架和传送带操作。消融实验表明，在更大的工作空间区域中，局部裁剪及其观察方向变得更加重要。

Conclusion: 结果表明，单个鱼眼相机即可支持这些操作任务，无需物理腕部相机，验证了以末端执行器为中心的局部视野分配策略和共享射线编码设计的有效性。

Abstract: Manipulation requires both broad scene awareness and detailed local feedback, yet conventional camera rigs provide them through separate front and wrist cameras. We present Fisheye-VLA, a visual interface that brings these capabilities together using a single passive fisheye. A global view preserves the workspace, while local perspective crops direct detail toward the interaction. The key design question is where this local visual budget should go. We answer it through a controlled re-rendering study, comparing alternative crop directions on the same recorded observations. The study finds that end-effector-centered views capture most of the estimated benefit of a much larger candidate pool, motivating a compact allocation around both hands. Our interface uses calibrated end-effector projection and motion lead to track the crops, while a shared ray encoding preserves their spatial meaning as they move. Integrated with a pretrained VLA, it achieves 84% and 82% success in the two expanded tabletop regions, where some target placements extend beyond the front-camera coverage, and supports shelf and conveyor manipulation. Ablations show that local crops and their viewing directions become more important in the larger workspace regions. The results demonstrate that a single fisheye can support these manipulation tasks without physical wrist cameras.

</details>


### [260] [PLAT: Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking for Humanoid Control via Privileged Latent Transition Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25754)
*Zepeng Wang,Jiangxing Wang,Chao Ma,Xiaochuan Shi,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PLAT 提出三阶段稀疏定时关键帧全身运动跟踪框架，训练时用稠密目标序列作特权监督、部署时仅需稀疏关键帧与到达时间，并在仿真与 Unitree G1 实体上验证了长时程下的稳定跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形运动跟踪策略依赖逐帧稠密参考，使其难以作为高层运动控制器用于规划与交互式运动生成；因此需要一种仅接收稀疏未来关键帧及其到达时间的跟踪策略，同时保持全身运动稳定并依次到达目标。

Method: PLAT 采用三阶段学习：1）预训练稠密跟踪专家以提供鲁棒运动先验；2）通过 DAgger 风格模仿学习特权潜在先验；3）进行潜在残差强化学习，在潜在转移层面进行精修而非直接优化动作。框架利用训练期稠密目标序列作为特权监督，桥接稠密跟踪与稀疏目标条件控制。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明 PLAT 在不同规划时程下均能保持准确稳定的稀疏定时关键帧跟踪，长时程指令下表现尤为突出；在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上的成功部署进一步验证了其有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: PLAT 通过特权潜在转移学习，将稠密运动跟踪与稀疏目标条件控制有效结合，为人形稀疏运动控制提供了可行的实用方案。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking policies rely on dense frame-by-frame references, limiting their use as high-level motion controllers for planning and interactive motion generation. We study \emph{Sparse Timed Keyframe Motion Tracking}, where a policy receives only sparse future keyframes and their desired arrival times, and must execute stable whole-body motions that reach successive goals. We propose \textbf{PLAT}, a three-stage sparse timed keyframe motion tracking policy learning framework with \textbf{P}rivileged \textbf{LA}tent \textbf{T}ransition learning. PLAT bridges dense motion tracking and sparse goal-conditioned control by exploiting dense goal sequences as privileged supervision during training while requiring only sparse timed keyframe commands at deployment. A pretrained dense tracking expert first provides robust motion priors. A privileged latent prior is then learned through DAgger-style imitation, followed by latent residual reinforcement learning that refines latent transitions instead of directly optimizing actions. Extensive simulation experiments demonstrate that PLAT maintains accurate and stable sparse timed keyframe tracking across varying planning horizons, with particularly strong performance under long-horizon commands. Successful deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot further demonstrates the effectiveness and practicality of PLAT for sparse humanoid motion control.

</details>


### [261] [MedVLA: A Hierarchical Vision-Language-Action Framework for Closed-Loop Precision Medical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25756)
*Junjie Xie,Chuxuan He,Angen Ye,Yujia Song,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MedVLA分层框架，将多模态推理与函数约束执行结合，用于精密医疗机器人，实现95%任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精密医疗机器人需要严格的安全、可解释和执行约束下的自适应决策。现有VLA模型的连续动作生成范式不适合精密医疗任务，且可靠闭环操作还可能依赖非动作的系统函数调用。

Method: MedVLA采用分层框架，耦合高层多模态推理与低层函数约束执行；并引入可扩展多智能体流水线生成面向技能的思维链（CoT）数据用于结构化训练，基于不同多模态大模型骨干进行微调。

Result: 在不同多模态大模型骨干上微调后性能一致提升。相同初始条件下进行100次闭环柔性电极植入试验，MedVLA达95.0%任务成功率，显著优于OpenVLA（8%）和π₀（15%），在准确性、稳定性和安全性上更优。

Conclusion: 结构化推理结合受约束的函数级执行，是通往可部署精密医疗机器人的一条切实可行路径。

Abstract: Precision medical robotics demands adaptive decision-making under strict safety, interpretability, and execution constraints. Although recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models show strong multimodal reasoning ability, their continuous action generation paradigm is not well suited for precision medical tasks, where reliable closed-loop operation may also depend on non-action system function calls. To address this gap, we propose MedVLA, a hierarchical framework that couples high-level multimodal reasoning with low-level function-constrained execution. We further introduce a scalable multi-agent pipeline to generate skill-oriented chain-of-thought(CoT) data for structured training. Built on different multimodal large-model backbones, MedVLA consistently improves performance after fine-tuning, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework across model variants. Under identical initial conditions, we perform 100 closed-loop flexible electrode implantation trials. The results show that MedVLA achieves a 95.0\% task success rate, substantially outperforming representative VLA baselines, including OpenVLA (8\%) and $π_0$ (15\%), in accuracy, stability, and safety. These results indicate that structured reasoning with constrained function-level execution is a practical route toward deployable precision medical robotics.

</details>


### [262] [VisForce: Visual Grounding of Current and Desired Forces for Goal-Conditioned Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25785)
*Jung-Woo Lee,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 VisForce，将当前力与目标力以视觉形式直接标注在对指尖位置，通过目标条件交叉注意力生成力感知动作，在真实灵巧手操作任务中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作中，接触力通常作为独立状态或力专用表示输入，难以显式表达"力"与"对应视觉位置"之间的空间对应关系，限制了 VLA 模型对力的精细利用。

Method: 将当前力和期望力渲染为对应指尖位置的视觉力提示（visual force cues），分别叠加在当前腕部图像和任务特定的目标图像上；再通过目标条件交叉注意力（goal-conditioned cross-attention）融合两种表示，生成力感知动作。

Result: 在真实 UR10 机器人 + RH56F1 灵巧手上评测：力条件抓取中，随期望力增大呈现一致的握力响应，鸡蛋和牙膏管的抓取提举成功率分别为 70% 和 80%；杯子插入/倒液、夹子辅助面包转移、滑动调制轴孔装配的最终成功率分别为 70%、55%、40%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，与指尖对齐的视觉力表示可有效用于基于 VLA 的灵巧手操作的力感知条件化。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as general-purpose robotic manipulation policies. However, in dexterous hand manipulation, contact forces are typically provided as separate states or force-specific representations, making it difficult to explicitly represent the spatial correspondence between force and their corresponding visual locations. In this work, we propose VisForce, which visually grounds the current and desired forces at their corresponding fingertip locations. VisForce renders current and desired visual force cues on the current wrist image and a task-specific goal image, and combines the two representations through goal-conditioned cross-attention to generate force-aware actions. We evaluate VisForce using a real UR10 robot equipped with an RH56F1 dexterous hand through force-conditioned grasping and three multi-stage manipulation tasks. In force-conditioned grasping experiments, VisForce exhibited a consistent grip-force response as the desired force increased, and achieved grasp-and-lift success rates of 70% and 80% for an egg and a toothpaste tube, respectively. It further achieved final success rates of 70%, 55%, and 40% on cup insertion/bottle pouring, tong-assisted bread transfer, and slip-modulated peg-in-hole, respectively. These results show that fingertip-aligned visual force representations can be effectively used for force-aware conditioning in VLA-based dexterous hand manipulation.

</details>


### [263] [MOLA LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (MOLA-LIO) on the COMFORT Localization Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25813)
*Jose Luis Blanco-Claraco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本报告记录了作者参加IROS 2026 COMFORT定位基准测试的方案，基于GrandTour数据集与Boxi载荷，将MOLA-LO扩展为融合IMU与可选腿部运动学里程计的激光雷达-惯性系统，并说明架构、输入流、配置选择协议与实时性测量依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与COMFORT Localization Benchmark（IROS 2026），在Boxi载荷采集的GrandTour数据集上验证定位性能，需要将原有的纯激光雷达里程计MOLA-LO提升为多传感器融合系统以应对该基准的定位需求。

Method: 在MOLA-LO基础上扩展为LiDAR-惯性系统，融合IMU数据，并可选地引入腿部运动学里程计（legged kinematic odometry）；采用本地协议进行配置筛选以确定最终提交配置。

Result: 报告描述了系统架构、所消费的数据流、筛选提交配置的本地协议，以及支撑实时性声明的测量结果（原文未给出具体数值）。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将MOLA-LO扩展为多传感器融合定位系统并用于COMFORT基准测试的可行性，并通过测量验证了系统的实时运行能力。

Abstract: This short report documents our entry to the COMFORT Localization Benchmark (IROS 2026), evaluated on the GrandTour dataset recorded with the Boxi payload. It extends MOLA-LO into a LiDAR-inertial system that also ingests IMU and, optionally, legged kinematic odometry. We describe the architecture, the streams consumed, the local protocol that selected the submitted configuration, and the measurements backing our real-time claim.

</details>


### [264] [Beyond Reconstruction Error: Analytical and Data-Driven Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25820)
*Yuxin Yang,Gaohan He,Changxue Guan,Hangming Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 动作离散化表征的重建保真度不能可靠预测闭环控制性能，需联合评估几何保真度、序列可预测性、解码器稳定性与闭环表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散动作分词是自回归VLA模型的核心，但现有动作表征多以重建保真度为主要评价标准，尚不清楚哪些表征属性真正决定闭环控制效果。

Method: 在统一的分词接口下，对比固定解析表征、数据驱动线性表征与非线性神经表征，开展率失真分析、序列建模诊断，并进行3,500次LIBERO仿真rollout实验，另设计种子匹配（seed-42）的消融实验。

Result: 表征排序随评价标准变化：PCA名义重建误差低于Temporal-DCT，但token序列可预测性更差，且在三个策略训练种子下见过任务平均成功率低3.0个百分点，某一随机种子下策略排序甚至反转；在匹配种子消融中，自编码器进一步降低重建误差却未带来最强策略，且对离散token扰动更敏感。

Conclusion: 仅靠重建保真度无法可靠筛选适合自回归控制的动作表征，应联合考量几何保真度、序列可预测性、解码器稳定性与闭环性能。

Abstract: Discrete action tokenization is central to autoregressive vision-language-action (VLA) models, yet action representations are often evaluated primarily through reconstruction fidelity. We ask which representation properties actually matter for closed-loop control by comparing fixed analytical, data-driven linear, and nonlinear neural representations under a unified tokenization interface. Across rate-distortion analysis, sequence-modeling diagnostics, and 3,500 LIBERO rollouts, representation rankings change with the evaluation criterion. PCA achieves lower nominal reconstruction error than Temporal-DCT, but produces less predictable token sequences and 3.0 percentage points lower mean seen-task success across three policy-training seeds, with the policy ordering reversing in one seed. In a matched seed-42 ablation, an autoencoder further reduces reconstruction error yet does not yield the strongest policy and exhibits greater sensitivity to discrete token perturbations. These findings show that reconstruction fidelity alone cannot reliably select action representations for autoregressive control, motivating joint evaluation of geometric fidelity, sequence predictability, decoder stability, and closed-loop performance.

</details>


### [265] [Sometimes You Gotta Run Before You Can Walk: Run-then-Walk Scheduling Strategy for VLM Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25831)
*Yuqi Ye,Shangkun Sun,Junhong Lin,Jiayi Zhao,Changhao Peng,Wei Zheng,Guoqing Liu,Tiesong Zhao,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Run-then-Walk 两阶段奖励调度策略用于 VLM 自动驾驶规划器的 GRPO 训练，先探索高进度、后修复安全性，在多个基准上提升性能并将 RL 训练轮数减少 40–50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 VLM 的自动驾驶规划器采用 GRPO 式强化学习优化驾驶表现，但存在两难：优化驾驶效率会导致追求进度却不安全的行为，而强制早期安全约束又会导致过度保守；两类方法都需冗长训练。作者旨在同时解决性能与收敛效率问题。

Method: 作者首先揭示两种不同的 RL 阶段：进度阶段（Run-GRPO，积极探索高进度）与安全阶段（Walk-GRPO，在稳定进度下恢复安全性）。据此提出 Run-then-Walk，一种简单有效的两阶段奖励调度：Run 阶段聚焦进度，使策略摆脱保守偏置、发现高进度模式；随后的 Walk 阶段引入端点与安全策略，修复 Run 阶段的不安全行为。该反向调度克服了先行走（Walk-first）方法的保守性和联合优化的不安全进度追求。

Result: 在 NAVSIMv1、NAVSIMv2、Navhard 和 nuScenes 等多个基准上，对多种基于 VLM 的规划器进行验证。大量实验表明，驾驶性能得到提升，同时所需 RL 训练轮数比基线减少 40–50%。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离进度发现与安全修复两个阶段，Run-then-Walk 兼顾了更优性能与更快收敛，为 VLM 自动驾驶规划器的 GRPO 训练提供了一种简单而有效的奖励调度方案。

Abstract: Recent VLM-based autonomous driving planners adopt GRPO-style reinforcement learning to optimize driving performance. However, existing GRPO recipes either optimize driving efficiency, risking progress-seeking but unsafe behavior, or enforce early safety constraints, leading to overly conservative behavior; both require lengthy training. To solve these problems, we first reveal two distinct RL regimes: a progress regime (Run-GRPO) that aggressively explores high progress, and a safety regime (Walk-GRPO) that restores safety under stable progress. Based on this finding, we propose $\textit{Run-then-Walk}$, a simple yet effective two-stage reward scheduling strategy for GRPO, achieving both better performance and faster convergence. Unlike one-stage RL, which may focus on progress, safety, or a mixture of both within a single training phase, this schedule explicitly separates progress discovery from safety repair. In the $\textit{Run}$ phase, we focus on progress, allowing the policy to escape the conservative bias and discover high-progress modes. In the subsequent $\textit{Walk}$ phase, we introduce endpoint and safety strategy to repair unsafe behaviors from the Run phase. This reversed schedule overcomes the conservatism of Walk-first methods and the unsafe progress-seeking of joint optimization. We validate it with various VLM-based planners on multiple benchmarks: NAVSIMv1, NAVSIMv2, Navhard, and nuScenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved driving performance while requiring 40--50\% fewer RL training epochs than the baselines.

</details>


### [266] [What is the Better Curriculum: Controller-Shaped Grasping Behavior for Contact Force-Sensitive Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25887)
*Ziyan Feng,Zizhao Yuan,Yulong Fu,Yuxin He,Zhiyuan Zhang,Zhengjie Zhang,Jinni Zhou,Renjing Xu,Qiang Nie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将25Hz确定性触觉反射控制器用作采集阶段的"教师"来生成演示数据，从而训练无触觉策略，用于学习受亚牛顿级接触力限制的易损物体抓取操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在直接接触的力敏感操作中，瓶颈往往出现在数据采集阶段而非策略本身：人工操作夹爪存在延迟且过于粗粒度，难以稳定维持抓取所需的狭窄力范围。现有视触觉策略学习通常将触觉作为策略输入，但本文认为在力敏感操作中触觉更应作用于采集阶段。

Method: 使用一个确定性的25Hz触觉反射控制器作为采集阶段的教师，生成具有"控制器塑形"抓取行为的演示数据，再用于无触觉策略学习；在ACT与π0.5两种策略上验证该反射塑形演示的效果，并与视觉筛选的人工演示以及部署时的反射仲裁器进行对比。

Result: 基于反射塑形演示训练的ACT策略能复现教师的抓取特征，在标称塑料杯任务上达到95%稳定抓取率，显著优于视觉筛选的人工演示；在π0.5上同样提升了同分布稳定性，并在未见过的纸杯变体上显示出积极的探索趋势。但在随机外部扰动下，仅靠反射数据的π0.5策略仍有45%失败率，而部署阶段的反射仲裁器可保持全部抓取成功。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈在力敏感操作中具有新角色：不再将其集成进策略，而是作为采集阶段的教师来塑造演示中的抓取行为以供策略学习；但扰动抑制仍依赖控制器，揭示了无触觉策略的能力边界。

Abstract: How should a robot learn to manipulate objects so fragile that sub-Newton contact forces can cause irreversible damage? Existing visuo-tactile policy learning typically treats tactile sensing as an additional policy input. In direct-contact force-sensitive manipulation, however, the bottleneck can arise earlier, during data collection: manual gripper control is too delayed and coarse-grained to reliably maintain the narrow force range required for stable grasping. We therefore use a deterministic 25 Hz tactile reflex controller as a collection-time teacher, producing demonstrations with controller-shaped grasping behavior for tactile-free policy learning. On Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), policies trained from reflex-shaped demonstrations recover the teacher's grasping profile and achieve 95% stable grasps on the nominal plastic-cup task, substantially outperforming visually screened manual demonstrations. The same intervention improves in-distribution stability on $π_{0.5}$ and shows a favorable exploratory trend on an unseen paper-cup variant. Under randomized external disturbance, however, the reflex-data $π_{0.5}$ policy still fails in 45% of policy-only trials, whereas a deployment-time reflex arbiter retains all grasps. These results reveal a new role for tactile feedback in force-sensitive manipulation: rather than integrating tactile into the policy, we use it as a collection-time teacher that shapes grasping behavior in demonstrations for policy learning, while disturbance rejection remains controller-dependent, revealing the boundary of tactile-free policy.

</details>


### [267] [Robust Active-Perception Control for Global-State-Free Aerial-Ground Cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25898)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Jiajun Yu,Baozhe Zhang,Pengxiang Zhou,Wentao Liu,Fei Gao,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出COPA主动感知框架，用单轴云台解耦相机光轴与无人机俯仰，结合TCN预测与MPC联合优化，实现无需全局状态的空地协同鲁棒跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地协同需要实时UAV-UGV相对状态信息。在UGV附着的非惯性系中直接控制可避免依赖全局定位，而基于被动视觉标记的相对位姿估计是低成本方案。但固定相机会在无人机姿态需求与视场约束冲突时丢失目标，因此需要主动感知来维持目标可见性。

Method: 使用单轴云台将相机光轴与无人机俯仰姿态解耦；建立关联无人机运动、云台角度与UGV运动到目标像平面状态的主动感知模型；用TCN从近期运动历史预测短时域UGV加速度与角速度（无需全局状态测量）；MPC利用这些预测联合优化无人机与云台控制。

Result: 仿真表明COPA能持续保持目标可见；消融实验证实TCN可降低UGV运动切换时的峰值误差；真实实验中UGV加速度达3m/s²、偏航角速度达1.0rad/s时仍实现鲁棒跟踪。

Conclusion: COPA通过云台主动感知与学习预测结合MPC，在无全局定位条件下实现了空地协同的连续视觉跟踪与鲁棒性能。

Abstract: Aerial-ground cooperation requires real-time UAV--UGV relative-state information. Instead of maintaining global estimates for both robots, direct control in a UGV-attached non-inertial frame avoids reliance on global localization. Vision-based relative pose estimation with a passive marker offers a low-cost and effective solution. However, a fixed camera may lose sight of the moving UGV when the required UAV attitude conflicts with the field-of-view (FOV) constraint. To address this, we propose COPA, a robust active-perception framework for global-state-free aerial-ground cooperation. We use a single-axis gimbal to decouple the camera optical axis from the UAV pitch attitude. We derive an active-perception model that relates UAV motion, gimbal angle, and UGV motion to the target image-plane state.A Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) predicts short-horizon UGV acceleration and angular velocity from recent motion history without global-state measurements. The model predictive control (MPC) uses these predictions to jointly optimize UAV and gimbal control. Simulations show that COPA maintains continuous target visibility, while ablation studies confirm that the TCN reduces peak errors during UGV motion transitions. Real-world experiments with UGV accelerations up to 3m/s^2 and yaw rates up to 1.0rad/s demonstrate robust tracking.

</details>


### [268] [You Should Be Properly Scoring Your Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25900)
*Ola Rønning,Usama Saqib,Andrzej Wąsowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用严格适当评分规则（strictly proper scoring rules）评估里程计性能，评分时同时考虑估计值与其协方差，并给出无真值情况下的成对检验方法，配套开源框架 smfeval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统里程计评估使用点度量（如RMSE），忽略了估计器已报告的协方差矩阵。协方差一方面编码估计器的不确定性、揭示其是否过度自信，另一方面可对误差方向加权，避免在不确定方向上对高误差的过度惩罚。

Method: 采用严格适当评分规则对估计值及其报告的不确定性联合评分；该类规则在无协方差时退化为点度量，在报告协方差时可诊断协方差不一致性。并使用单边成对检验，在无真值条件下检测两个估计器中至少一个的过度自信。相关方法在开源框架 smfeval 中实现。

Result: 以地基 LiDAR-惯性里程计的平移分量为案例，在四种滤波器上均发现过度自信，最差情形报告厘米级确定性却存在千米级误差。进一步溯源发现，过度自信源于滤波器赋予 LiDAR 测量的新信息量超过其实际携带量。

Conclusion: 评估里程计应使用严格适当评分规则而非点度量，以纳入并检验协方差质量；该方法可在无真值时诊断过度自信，并帮助揭示过度自信的机制。相关工具已开源为 smfeval。

Abstract: When we evaluate the performance of our odometry, it is common practice to score the estimated track against a ground truth. Unfortunately, scoring uses point metrics, such as the root mean square error, that ignore the covariance matrix which estimators like filters and smoothers already report. Using the covariance matters for two reasons. First, the covariance encodes the estimator's uncertainty, so it tells us whether the estimator trusts its own output. An overconfident estimator will not report itself lost. Second, the covariance weights the error in each direction of the estimate. Without the covariance, an estimator is unduly penalized for a high error in an uncertain direction. Instead of point metrics, we should use strictly proper scoring rules. These rules score the estimate together with its reported uncertainty. Strictly proper scoring rules recover the point metrics when no covariance is reported, and they diagnose covariance inconsistency when covariance is reported. Using a one-sided pairwise test, we show that two estimators can expose overconfidence in at least one of them without a ground truth. Strictly proper scoring rules and our pairwise test are available in our open-source framework smfeval. As a case study, we use smfeval to assess the uncertainty quality of the translational component of ground-based LiDAR-inertial odometry. Across four filters we find overconfidence - the worst case reports centimeter certainty with kilometer error. Knowing the filters are overconfident, we investigate the mechanism. The investigation traces overconfidence to filters crediting LiDAR measurements with more new information than they carry.

</details>


### [269] [Control Barrier Functions for Safe Free-Flying Robotic Spacecraft Operations in Tumbling Target Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25905)
*Alexander Meinert,Peter Stadler,Niklas Baldauf,Alen Turnwald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个模块化控制障碍函数（CBF）框架，用于翻滚目标捕获过程中自由飞行航天器机器人的安全操作，并通过高保真仿真验证了其实时性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受欧空局（ESA）关于安全近距离操作的最新指南启发，翻滚目标捕获任务需要将安全区域与要求形式化，并保证多子系统耦合下的系统级安全。

Method: 将13自由度系统分解为平移、姿态与机器人子系统；每个子系统配备安全滤波器，通过轻量级二次规划以最小方式修改标称控制输入；滤波器约束包括锥形接近走廊、碰撞避免区、姿态视线指向、角速度限制、关节限制、连杆-本体碰撞避免及执行器约束；子系统间的动态耦合通过把上游安全控制指令视为下游滤波器的已知互联输入来处理。

Result: 在轨服务场景（含最终逼近、角速度同步与翻滚目标抓取）中，基于高保真天体动力学仿真器Basilisk进行验证；蒙特卡洛仿真表明该框架在不同翻滚速率下具备运行时效率与操作安全性。

Conclusion: 该模块化CBF框架能够在保持模块性的同时支持系统级安全，适用于翻滚目标捕获等自由飞行航天器机器人的安全控制任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a modular control barrier function (CBF) framework for safe free-flying robotic spacecraft operations during tumbling target capture. Motivated by latest ESA guidelines for safe close proximity operations, safety zones and requirements are translated into dedicated CBFs. The 13-DoF system is decomposed into translational, attitude, and robotic subsystems, each equipped with a safety filter that minimally modifies nominal control inputs in a lightweight quadratic program. The filters enforce a conical approach corridor, collision avoidance zone, attitude line-of-sight pointing, angular velocity limits, robotic joint limits, link-base collision avoidance, and actuator constraints. Dynamic coupling between subsystems is handled by treating upstream safe control commands as known interconnection inputs in the downstream safety filters, preserving modularity while supporting system-level safety. The framework is validated in an on-orbit servicing scenario, including final approach, angular rate synchronization, and tumbling target grasping, using the high-fidelity astrodynamics simulator Basilisk. Monte Carlo simulation results demonstrate runtime efficiency and operational safety for various tumbling rates.

</details>


### [270] [Vision-based Underwater Formation Control With Input Saturations via Barrier Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25917)
*Nicola De Carli,João Zenário,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需通信的视觉编队控制框架，用于全驱动水下机器人，在感知约束、避碰要求和输入饱和条件下实现编队控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人编队控制面临感知受限、碰撞风险和执行器饱和等实际约束，且通信受限场景下难以依赖机器人间通信，需要一种仅基于视觉的解决方案。

Method: 采用重心化障碍李雅普诺夫函数（recentered barrier Lyapunov functions）编码感知与避碰约束；用命令滤波反步法将设计扩展到二阶载体动力学；通过二次规划（QP）显式处理执行器限制；采用保守感知域预留安全裕度，并在必要时自适应放宽，允许临时越界。

Result: 在Gazebo中通过真实的软件在环（SITL）仿真验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该通信无关的视觉编队控制框架能同时应对感知约束、避碰和输入饱和，并通过仿真得到验证。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a communication-free framework for vision-based formation control of fully actuated underwater robots subject to sensing constraints, collision-avoidance requirements, and input saturations. Recentered barrier Lyapunov functions encode sensing and collision-avoidance constraints, while command-filtered backstepping extends the design to the second-order vehicle dynamics. The resulting control objective is enforced through a quadratic program that explicitly accounts for actuator limits. Conservative sensing domains provide margins from the physical limits and are adaptively relaxed when necessary, allowing temporary violation of the conservative bounds. The proposed approach is validated through realistic Software-in-the-Loop (SITL) simulations in Gazebo.

</details>


### [271] [Unsigned Distance Maps on 2D Point Cloud Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25932)
*Ricardo B. Sousa,Giorgio Grisetti,Héber Miguel Sobreira,Carlos André Silva,António Paulo Moreira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于无符号距离图（unsigned distance map）的2D点云配准方法，通过预计算最近参考点的欧氏距离及其空间导数于离散网格上，将每轮迭代的最近邻搜索替换为O(1)查表，并在SE(2)流形上推导点到点与点到面误差并用Gauss-Newton求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2D点云配准在移动机器人激光里程计与SLAM中十分重要，ICP虽广泛使用，但其迭代过程每轮都需通过最近邻搜索重算对应关系；而无需对应关系的替代方案多局限于scan-to-map对齐。因此作者希望避免逐次搜索的计算开销，同时保持配准精度。

Method: 该方法基于无符号距离图：在离散网格上预计算到最近参考点的欧氏距离及其空间导数，用O(1)查表替代逐迭代的最近邻搜索。随后在SE(2)流形上推导点到点（point-to-point）与点到面（point-to-plane）误差公式，并通过Gauss-Newton优化求解。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界IILABS 3D数据集上，预计算的点到点变体优于其解析对应版本，在激光里程计漂移方面取得了与点到面方法相当的表现。作者指出，预计算梯度在存在传感器噪声时对对应关系起到了正则化作用。

Conclusion: 预计算距离图能够以O(1)查表高效替代ICP的逐迭代最近邻搜索，并且基于无符号距离图的点到点公式在真实噪声场景下具有竞争力，其预计算梯度对对应关系具有正则化效果。

Abstract: 2D point cloud registration arises in laser odometry and Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) for mobile robots. Iterative Closest Point (ICP) is one of the most widely used approaches. Still, its iterative procedure recomputes correspondences via nearest-neighbor search at every iteration, whereas correspondence-free alternatives focus on scan-to-map alignment. This paper proposes a 2D point cloud registration approach based on unsigned distance maps, precomputing the Euclidean distance to the nearest reference point, along with its spatial derivatives, over a discrete grid, replacing the per-iteration search with O(1) lookups. Moreover, point-to-point and point-to-plane error formulations are derived on the SE(2) manifold and solved via Gauss-Newton optimization. On a synthetic benchmark and the real-world IILABS 3D dataset, the precomputed point-to-point variant outperforms its analytical counterparts, achieving competitive laser-odometry drift compared to point-to-plane formulations, as the precomputed gradient regularizes correspondences in the presence of sensor noise.

</details>


### [272] [Destination Support Restoration for Finite-Set Multimodal Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25942)
*Fengrui Liu,Jiajun Peng,Duo Peng,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Destination Support Restoration (DSR)，一种因果后选择算子，用于修复行人轨迹预测中有限假设集的目的地覆盖不足问题，在不重训练、不扩大集合规模的前提下改善预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在行人周围作业时，通常依赖有限的预测未来集合进行决策。在线反复更新会使有限的预测预算集中于主导目的地，导致合理的替代目的地被稀释或缺失，从而使下游决策可用的有限表示不完整。因此需要一种在不重训练宿主预测器、不增加维护集合大小的前提下修复目的地支持的方法。

Method: DSR 是一种因果后选择算子。在每个修复步骤中，它从观测前缀构建临时按目的地分层的候选库，将候选证据转换为整数目标计数，保护活跃模式的代表，并把冗余的剩余假设重新分配到覆盖不足的模式上。维护集与返回集严格保持 N 个假设，每次最多替换 ⌈ρN⌉ 个条目。被保护的代表的类别支持得到保留，血统感知粒子滤波还保留存活的 resampling 祖先。每次替换都会使分配与证据驱动目标之间的不匹配减少一个单位。

Result: 在包含 3,719 条轨迹的 Edinburgh 协议、三个随机种子的完整实验中，N=64 时 DSR 将 MIF 加权 ADE 和 FDE 分别降低 13.36% 和 13.30%。与 CLiFF、PPT、causal GDTS、Social Informer 和 PECNet 的成对集成在每一对评估中都同时改善了这两项指标。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当固定假设集作为下游系统接口时，有限集合的支持分配是一个有用的预测侧控制点。

Abstract: Robots operating around pedestrians often reason over a finite set of predicted human futures. Repeated online updates can concentrate this limited prediction budget on dominant destinations and leave plausible alternatives underrepresented or absent, removing those alternatives from the finite representation available to downstream decision making. We introduce Destination Support Restoration (DSR), a causal post-selection operator that repairs destination support without retraining the host predictor or increasing the maintained set size. At a repair step, DSR evaluates a temporary destination-stratified candidate bank from the observed prefix, converts candidate evidence into integer target counts, protects representatives of active modes, and reallocates redundant surplus hypotheses to deficient modes. The maintained and returned sets retain exactly $N$ hypotheses, and DSR replaces at most $\lceilρN\rceil$ entries. Protected representatives preserve current categorical support; lineage-aware particle filters also preserve surviving resampling ancestors. Each replacement reduces the allocation mismatch to the evidence-driven target by one. On the complete 3,719-trajectory Edinburgh protocol over three seeds, DSR reduces MIF weighted ADE and FDE by 13.36% and 13.30% at $N=64$. Paired integrations with CLiFF, PPT, causal GDTS, Social Informer, and PECNet improve both metrics in every evaluated pair. These results show that finite-set support allocation is a useful prediction-side control point when a fixed hypothesis set serves as the interface to downstream systems.

</details>


### [273] [An Action Is Worth One Patch: Unified World-Action Modeling with PatchWAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25961)
*Tianheng Wang,Zhou Xie,Heng Jia,Jianhua Xu,Tong Zhang,Kaicheng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PatchWAM，将连续动作视为另一种 patch，用单一生成式模型统一视觉预测与动作生成，无需独立动作头或动作专家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常引入可训练动作头或独立动作专家，来连接低维状态与高维视觉表示，这暗示视觉预测与动作生成可能需要分离的计算路径。本文试图探究视觉主干已有的建模能力是否也能直接支持控制。

Method: 提出 Action-as-Patch 固定映射，将连续动作表达为与视觉 patch 兼容的表示，使其成为同类 patch；并据此构建 PatchWAM（Patch World-Action Model），让单一模型在同一生成过程中同时预测机器人运动与场景未来变化，不设专用动作头或动作专家。

Result: 在子采样训练窗口实验中优于匹配的双专家控制方案；完整数据设置并加入额外增强示范后，在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到 91.8% 成功率，在 RoboTwin 2.0 上达到 96.12%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，能力不必在缺乏处新增，而可以被继承：扩展生成式主干的约束在于新信号的接口形式，而非建模容量本身。

Abstract: Generative visual models offer a foundation for learning representations of physical dynamics, yet their extension to continuous control raises a fundamental question: do visual prediction and action generation require separate computational pathways? Existing approaches usually introduce trainable action heads or separate action experts to bridge low-dimensional states and high-dimensional visual representations. In this work, we explore whether the visual backbone's existing capacity can also support control when actions are expressed in a compatible representation. Thus, we introduce PatchWAM (Patch World-Action Model), which treats continuous actions as another type of patch through a fixed mapping called Action-as-Patch. This allows a single model to predict both how the robot should move and what the scene may look like afterward. Visual prediction and action generation become parts of the same generative process, without a dedicated action head or separate action expert. Experiments with subsampled training windows show gains over a matched dual-expert control, while benchmark evaluations reach 91.8% success rate on LIBERO-Plus and 96.12% on RoboTwin 2.0 in a full-data setting with additional augmented demonstrations. More broadly, the result suggests that capability need not be added where it can be inherited: the constraint on extending a generative backbone is the interface a new signal is written in, not the capacity to model it.

</details>


### [274] [Predict Before You Step: Auditable Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Obstacle Avoidance under Sparse Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25969)
*Yuhui Mao,Fen Liu,Shenghai Yuan,Tianxin Hu,Ruimeng Liu,Rong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LOOP局部避障策略，利用循环占用预测网络在稀疏路点引导下实现四足机器人动态避障，仿真超越基线，实机部署可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在稀疏路点引导下需依靠部分且快速变化的LiDAR观测躲避移动障碍物，现有反应式策略缺乏对未来占据的预测能力，导致迎面与交叉场景表现受限。

Method: 从占用与自速度历史出发，用循环预测器以学习到的光流和可见性门控对当前地图进行形变，预测1秒未来的占用地图；通过地图导出的特征与几何风险估计进行速度选择，形成可检查、可替换的显式接口，并以50Hz连接稀疏路点引导与冻结的运动控制器。

Result: 在Isaac Lab遭遇同步评测中，2.5-3.2 m/s障碍速度下迎面成功率达57.1%，比重新训练的反应式基线高8.2个百分点；与无rollout的BEV策略相比，预测分支带来场景相关的增益（交叉成功率提升、最高迎面速度下种子间方差降低）；适配器在Unitree Go2上每步14.5 ms，16次真实交叉试验全部无碰撞。

Conclusion: LOOP通过显式的未来占用预测接口有效提升动态避障性能，并具备实机部署的可行性。

Abstract: Legged robots under sparse waypoint guidance must avoid moving obstacles using partial, rapidly changing LiDAR observations. We present LOOP (Latent-recurrent Occupancy rollOut Policy), a local avoidance policy that connects sparse waypoint guidance to a frozen locomotion controller at 50 Hz. From occupancy and ego-velocity histories, a recurrent predictor forecasts future occupancy over a 1 s horizon by warping the current map with learned flow and visibility gates. These maps guide velocity selection through map-derived features and geometric risk estimates, providing an explicit interface for inspecting and replacing predictions. In encounter-synchronised Isaac Lab evaluations, LOOP achieves 57.1% head-on success at obstacle speeds of 2.5-3.2 m/s, exceeding a retrained reactive baseline by 8.2 percentage points. Comparisons with a rollout-free BEV policy show smaller, scenario-dependent gains from the prediction branch, including improved crossing success and reduced variability across training seeds at the highest head-on speeds. The adapter runs onboard a Unitree Go2 in 14.5 ms per step and completes all 16 real-world crossing trials without collision, demonstrating deployment feasibility.

</details>


### [275] [Safety-Constrained Model Predictive Control for an Omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot Using Control Barrier Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25994)
*Andrea Fortuna,Marta Lorenzini,Elisa Motta,Alberto Ranavolo,Elena De Momi,Arash Ajoudani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将控制障碍函数（CBF）融入模型预测控制（MPC）的框架，用于全向助行机器人I-WANDER，在优化能效与协作平滑性的同时显式保证避障安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为运动障碍者提供安全有效的助行辅助对恢复其独立性和生活质量至关重要，而机器人助行设备需在提供顺应性交互的同时确保用户安全与支撑，因此需要兼顾安全约束与能效、平滑协作的控制方法。

Method: 提出CBF-based MPC（CB-MPC）控制框架，将控制障碍函数集成进模型预测控制中以显式施加避碰安全约束，并优化能效与人机协作平滑性；与可变导纳控制器（AC）对比，招募12名健康受试者完成两项任务：U形走廊结构化导航任务，以及蒙眼进行的单障碍避障任务（以保证障碍出现是意外的）。

Result: 对比结果表明，CB-MPC架构显著降低了能耗与机械做功（p < 0.01），且未损害运动平滑性，同时减少了障碍碰撞次数。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了所提控制架构在提升机器人助行辅助的安全性与效率方面的潜力。

Abstract: Providing safe and effective mobility assistance plays a crucial role in restoring independence and enhancing the quality of life for individuals with motor impairments. In this context, robotic walking assistive devices have recently emerged as promising solutions to provide physically compliant interaction while ensuring user safety and support. This paper presents a novel control framework for an omnidirectional Walking Assistive Robot (I-WANDER) that integrates a Control Barrier Function (CBF) formulation into a Model Predictive Control (MPC) scheme to explicitly enforce collision-avoidance safety constraints while optimizing for energy efficiency and smooth human-robot collaboration. The method was experimentally evaluated with 12 healthy participants performing two different walking tasks using both the proposed CBF-based MPC controller (CB-MPC) and a variable admittance controller (AC). The first task involved structured navigation through a U-shaped corridor, whereas the second consisted of a single-obstacle avoidance task performed blindfolded to ensure the obstacle was unexpected. Comparative results show that the CB-MPC architecture significantly reduces energy consumption and mechanical work (p < 0.01) without compromising motion smoothness, while also decreasing the number of obstacle collisions. Overall, the findings highlight the potential of the proposed control architecture to enhance both safety and efficiency in robotic walking assistance.

</details>


### [276] [Manipulation with Stability Guarantees: Linear Deformable Objects with Non-negligible Physical Response Grasped at Multiple Location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26004)
*Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将可变形物体操作从准静态假设扩展到动力学范畴，通过SE(3)驱动坐标与非线性反馈实现闭环形状调节，仿真与实验均验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体操作研究多针对轻量、力学响应可忽略的系统，实质局限于准静态工况，因而排除了软管、管道、线束等动力学不可忽略的实用对象。

Method: 提出闭环控制架构，将操作重构为形状调节问题，通过在物体多个固定点施加力与力矩进行控制；方法上包含三项贡献：基于离散应变参数化的线性可变形物体全动力学模型；将驱动坐标扩展到SE(3)的控制架构；以及给出稳态收敛显式条件的非线性反馈策略。

Result: 在代表性操作任务上的大量仿真显示所提模型驱动方法带来性能提升；并通过带在线形状估计的实时闭环实现完成实验验证，证实了实际可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了准静态限制，为动力学显著的可变形线性物体操作提供了结构化、欠驱动且可收敛的闭环控制方案，兼具仿真与实验支持。

Abstract: Most research on the manipulation of deformable objects focuses on lightweight systems with negligible mechanical response, effectively restricting attention to quasi-static regimes. This assumption excludes a broad class of practically relevant objects, such as hoses, pipes, and wiring harnesses, whose dynamics cannot be ignored during manipulation. In this work, we address this limitation by introducing a closed-loop control architecture that explicitly accounts for object dynamics and recasts manipulation as a shape-regulation problem. Control is achieved by modulating forces and torques applied at multiple fixed points along the object. This approach builds on three methodological contributions: a fully dynamic model of linear deformable objects based on discrete strain parameterizations; an extension of the notion of actuation coordinates to SE(3), yielding a structured and inherently underactuated control architecture; and nonlinear feedback strategies providing explicit conditions for steady-state convergence to desired configurations. Extensive simulations on representative manipulation tasks demonstrate the performance gains enabled by the proposed modelbased formulation. We finally validate the approach experimentally through a real-time closed-loop implementation with online shape estimation, confirming its practical feasibility and effectiveness

</details>


### [277] [Skytopia: Monocular Drone Navigation with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26007)
*Yuhang Zhang,Rangya Zhang,Yujing Shang,Zhuoyuan Yu,Weiying Wang,Steven Yang,Qingsong Yan,Chao Yan,Mir Feroskhan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 skytopia，一个基于动作条件潜在世界模型的无人机导航策略，配合用于训练的 3D 高斯泼溅平台。该策略在部署时丢弃预测器，仅保留表征，从而统一支持点目标、图像目标和目标无关导航，并在仿真与真实无人机上取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目无人机导航需在未知环境中仅凭单个前向相机到达目标，但该设置提供的深度和尺度线索极少。世界模型通过建模观测在动作下的演化来应对，但传统世界模型是为"执行"而构建的：预测在部署时生成并逐步反馈进动作生成，计算开销大。作者质疑策略真正需要的并非预测本身，而是产生预测所需的表征——因为飞行中执行的动作几乎解释了观测间全部变化，预测可简化为已知位移下静态场景的重投影。

Method: 引入 skytopia：一个建立在动作条件潜在世界模型之上的策略，以及用于训练它的 3D 高斯泼溅平台。方法包含两个目标：前向目标从预期运动预测下一观测的表征；逆向目标从预测的转移中恢复该运动。由于预测不进入动作生成，预测器被丢弃，单一策略同时服务点目标、图像目标和目标无关三种导航设定。

Result: 仿真实验表明，skytopia 在三种设定下均优于所有基线，成功率分别达到 57.8%、66.0% 和 49.0%；丢弃预测器可减少 59.4% 的推理成本。同一策略随后无需微调即部署到真实无人机，在室内、开阔室外和林地环境中成功到达目标。

Conclusion: 工作指出策略从世界模型中真正需要的是表征而非预测，据此设计的 skytopia 在仿真中全面超越基线并显著降低推理成本，且能零微调迁移到真实无人机，验证了该范式的有效性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Monocular drone navigation requires reaching a goal in an unseen environment from a single forward-facing camera, which offers few cues for depth and scale. World models address this by modelling how observations evolve under actions, but they are built to be executed: the prediction is produced at deployment and fed back into action generation at every control step. We argue that what a policy needs from a world model is not the prediction but the representation required to produce it: in flight the executed action explains almost all of the change between observations, so prediction reduces to reprojecting a static scene under a known displacement. We therefore introduce skytopia, a policy built on an action-conditioned latent world model, and the 3D Gaussian Splatting platform on which it is trained. A forward objective predicts the representation of the next observation from the intended motion, and an inverse objective recovers that motion from the predicted transition. Because the prediction never reaches action generation, the predictor is discarded and one policy serves point-goal, image-goal, and goal-free navigation. Simulation experiments show that skytopia outperforms every baseline under all three specifications, attaining 57.8%, 66.0%, and 49.0% success rate, while discarding the predictor removes 59.4% of the inference cost. The same policy is subsequently deployed on a physical drone without fine-tuning and reaches goals in indoor, open outdoor, and woodland environments.

</details>


### [278] [Towards Intent-Aware Human-Robot Teaming: A Platform for Search-and-Rescue Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26051)
*Rohith Prem Maben,Ayesha Jena,Björn Olofsson,Stefan Reitmann,Jacek Malec,Rogier Woltjer,Elin Anna Topp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个人机协作交互平台，用于研究复杂动态环境下操作员与异构自主智能体的协作，并支持意图推断与决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂、动态的环境中，人类操作员与异构自主智能体之间的有效协作面临诸多挑战，需要可研究操作员行为并支持意图推断与决策的交互平台。

Method: 构建了一个交互平台，允许研究操作员行为，并采用前沿框架支持意图推断与决策，用于探究人机协作机制。

Result: 通过搜救场景验证，展示了自主系统能在多大程度上支持操作员的感知、决策与行动。

Conclusion: 该平台可有效支撑人机协作研究，并证明自主系统能够显著辅助操作员在复杂任务中的感知、决策与行动。

Abstract: We investigate the challenges of enabling effective collaboration between human operators and heterogeneous autonomous agents in complex, dynamic environments by developing an interaction platform that allows study of operator behavior and supports intent inference and decision-making using state-of-the-art frameworks. We demonstrate the extent to which the operator's perception, decisions, and actions could be supported by autonomous systems during search-and-rescue operations with our platform.

</details>


### [279] [StrataVLA: Hierarchical and Efficient 3D Geometric Grounding for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26071)
*Jin Cui,Zhaoyu Pu,Botao Cai,Jun Ye,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StrataVLA 是一种即插即用框架，通过层次化几何接地，将冻结几何基础模型的几何特征在多个骨干深度注入 VLA 模型，在提升操作成功率的同时大幅降低几何模型调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型虽继承强语义先验，但 3D 空间感知不足；已有方法要么需要显式深度或点云输入，要么把几何压缩为训练监督，或仅在输入/动作专家处注入，导致视觉-语言骨干缺乏持续可用的任务相关空间信息。

Method: 冻结的几何基础模型从 RGB 观测中提取共享几何特征；稀疏、层特定的 Geometry Adapters 让选定骨干深度的视觉表示通过交叉注意力检索相关几何证据；推理时结合任务感知路由与 LRU 特征缓存，以利用任务操作中的时间冗余。

Result: 在 LIBERO、SimplerEnv 及真实世界操作任务上均优于强 VLA 基线；LIBERO 套件平均成功率 98.53%，几何模型调用次数最多减少 88%。

Conclusion: 层次化几何注入是实现空间接地机器人控制的一种有效且高效的方式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models inherit strong semantic priors from large-scale vision-language pretraining, yet remain limited in robotic manipulation by insufficient 3D spatial awareness. Existing approaches either require explicit depth or point-cloud inputs, compress geometry into training-time supervision, or inject it only at the model input or action expert, leaving the vision-language backbone without persistent access to task-relevant spatial information. We introduce StrataVLA, a plug-and-play framework for hierarchical geometric grounding. A frozen geometry foundation model extracts shared geometric features from RGB observations, while sparse, layer-specific Geometry Adapters allow visual representations at selected backbone depths to retrieve relevant geometric evidence through cross-attention. To make inference-time geometry practical, StrataVLA further combines task-aware routing with an LRU feature cache that exploits temporal redundancy during task manipulation. Experiments on LIBERO, SimplerEnv, and real-world manipulation demonstrate consistent gains over strong VLA baselines. StrataVLA achieves 98.53% average success on LIBERO suites while reducing geometry-model invocations by up to 88%, establishing hierarchical geometry injection as an effective and efficient way to achieve spatially grounded robotic control.

</details>


### [280] [Situation Aware Locomotion for Dual Mobile Cobots in Shared Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26083)
*William Moraes,Igor Nunes,Ahilen Mazondo,Sebastian Barcelona,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向共享工业环境的双移动协作机器人态势感知运动框架，通过感知、理解、预测进行运动决策，仿真中任务成功率100%，显著优于两个基线（33.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两台移动协作机器人在共享工业环境中作业时，存在共享区域占用、障碍物、机器人间冲突等问题，独立决策或固定优先级策略难以保证安全与效率，因此需要引入态势感知来支撑安全运动决策。

Method: 以感知、理解、预测三个层次建模态势感知；输入特征包括机器人位姿、负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区域占用、障碍物状态及预测的机器人间冲突，据此选择安全运动动作；在仿真中实现，场景按4m×4m实体测试区设计，并与两种基线在多轮次、随机种子下进行对比。

Result: 所提态势感知方法在所有场景中任务成功率达100%，而独立基线与固定优先级基线总体成功率均为33.3%；该方法消除了共享区域冲突与安全停机，保持了最大的平均最小机器人间距离，并以最低平均完成时间完成任务。

Conclusion: 态势感知可有效提升双机器人运动表现，其关键在于融合负载状态、机械臂状态、共享区域推理与短时域冲突预测。

Abstract: This paper presents a situation aware locomotion framework for two mobile collaborative robots operating in shared industrial environments. The proposed method models situation awareness through perception, comprehension, and projection to support locomotion decisions. Robot pose, load state, manipulator state, shared zone occupancy, obstacle state, and predicted inter robot conflict were used to select safe locomotion actions. The framework was implemented in simulation and evaluated in simulated industrial scenarios designed to match a feasible 4 m by 4 m physical test area. The proposed method was compared with two other baselines over multiple trials and randomized seeds. The results show that the situation aware method achieved 100% task success across all scenarios, while the independent and fixed priority baselines each achieved 33.3% overall success. The proposed method eliminated shared zone conflicts and safety stops, maintained the largest average minimum inter robot distance, and completed the tasks with the lowest average completion time. These results indicate that situational awareness can improve the locomotion of dual robots by combining load state, manipulator state, reasoning about the shared zone, and prediction of short-horizon conflicts.

</details>


### [281] [Vision-Language Models as copilots for Autonomous UAV Navigation: Analysis of Latency and Reliability in Degraded Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26084)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Ahilen Mazondo,Igor Nunes,William Moraes,André Kelbouscas,Ricardo Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合FSM-VLM控制架构，用于无GPS环境下无人机的自主导航，通过SITL仿真评估不同参数规模VLM的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时闭环无人机导航对视觉语言模型（VLM）的要求不仅是低推理延迟，还需服从结构化飞行指令。当前VLM在自主飞行集成中面临格式层面的语法错误和逻辑层面的语义幻觉问题，需要明确其瓶颈所在。

Method: 提出混合FSM-VLM控制架构：确定性有限状态机（FSM）负责底层物理控制，异步VLM副驾驶负责高层语义路径规划。在软件在环（SITL）仿真中评估三种不同参数规模的模型，在正常与退化场景下分别隔离并测量格式层面的语法错误与逻辑层面的语义幻觉。

Result: 在正常与退化两种场景下，成功隔离并测量了格式级语法错误与逻辑级语义幻觉两类错误；跨三种参数规模模型的对比显示参数规模而非纯延迟是主要影响因素。

Conclusion: 参数规模、而非纯延迟，仍是VLM安全且兼容地集成到自主飞行中的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: The integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offers unprecedented semantic reasoning capabilities. However, real-time closed-loop navigation requires not only low inference latency but also obedience to structured flight commands. This paper proposes a hybrid FSM-VLM control architecture for UAVs in GPS-free environments. The system combines a deterministic Finite State Machine (FSM) for low-level physical control with an asynchronous VLM copilot for high-level semantic pathfinding. We evaluate three models with different parameter scales in a Software-In-The-Loop (SITL) simulation. The framework isolates and measures syntax errors at the format level versus semantic hallucinations at the logic level in a normal and degraded scenario. This study demonstrates that parameter scaling, and not pure latency, remains the primary bottleneck for the safe and compatible integration of VLM into autonomous flights.

</details>


### [282] [Acoustic Ellipses: Bio-Inspired Omnidirectional Echolocation in Cooperative Multi-Agent Systems using Frequency Sweeps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26085)
*Petras Swissler,Lindsay Burke,Julia Hyland Bruno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受鸟类飞行与鸣唱启发，提出一种利用固定声源发出调频chirp信号、运动听者接收直达与反射信号来识别环境障碍物的协同定位与建图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为蜂群机器人系统提供一种低成本的环境建图方案，模仿鸟类利用声音感知环境的机制。

Method: 固定源代理发出频率调制chirp，一个或多个运动听者代理观测直达与反射信号；利用直达与反射chirp信号间的"频率间隙"定义候选反射椭圆，再通过2D累积滤波器找出潜在反射密度最高的位置。先用自制高效音频仿真器进行仿真，考察障碍物数量、采样率和路径曲率对单听者场景识别精度的影响，随后分析双听者配置下的不同协同运动策略，最后在户外公园用现成硬件进行实地验证。

Result: 仿真结果显示障碍物数量、采样率与路径曲率会影响障碍识别准确性；不同协同运动策略在双听者配置下表现不同；实地实验在户外公园环境中利用现成硬件成功识别出频率间隙，验证了方法的现实可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究为蜂群机器人系统中的低成本环境建图奠定了基础。

Abstract: Inspired by the flight and song of birds, we propose an approach that enables cooperating agents to effectively identify obstacles in their environment by having a stationary source agent emit a frequency-modulated chirp while one or more listener agents observe the direct and reflected signals while in motion. We present a novel mapping approach that exploits the ``frequency gap'' between direct and reflected chirp signals to define candidate reflection ellipses, which are then fed into a 2D accumulation filter to identify locations with the highest density of potential reflections.
  We demonstrate this work first with simulation results derived from an efficient, bespoke audio simulator, examining the effect of obstacle count, sampling rate, and path curvature on the ability to accurately identify environmental obstacles for a one-listener scenario. We then examine different cooperative motion strategies for two-listener configurations.
