For each clean-correct sample, the evaluation records the clean pairwise margin, the first-order linearized margin-drop scale, the margin drop from a clean-start one-step attack, and the drop reached by an iterative attack. Normalizing by that scale gives three margin-drop coordinates capturing clean margin slack, one-step shortfall, and drift, where drift is the additional normalized margin drop the iterative attack reaches beyond the one-step perturbation. Together, they reconstruct the normalized post-attack margin and therefore the pass-or-fail outcome. Across 42 pretrained frozen vision encoders, the shallow survival rate carries essentially no rank information about subsequent PGD-10 to PGD-200 collapse, at Spearman -0.006, while the median shallow drift coordinate ranks the same collapse at +0.811. The result persists in a held-out encoder pool and under an $\ell_\infty$ evaluation. With deep evaluation limited to 11 encoders, ranking by shallow drift recovers 11 of the 17 high-collapse encoders, compared with 5 under survival-rate ranking. The full coordinate decomposition further distinguishes cases that share the same fixed-budget residual but diverge at deeper budgets, and separates margin repair from drift repair under interventions, revealing distinct repair paths that endpoint robustness alone does not identify.

</details>


### [390] [CoEvo: Oracle-Grounded Self-Evolution of a Single Model for Multi-Step Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26094)
*Jian Zhang,Bingyi Wang,Yizhi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoEvo，一种基于可查询 oracle 的自进化框架，让单个模型在 Proposer 与 Solver 角色间交替，实现多步因果推理的过程级自我监督与课程进化，8B 模型在多个高价值领域基准上超越蒸馏基线与最强专有参考的路径正确率（82.1% vs. 71.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步因果推理需逐步约束，早期误差会静默传播，且"答案对但逻辑错"无法被结果级检测发现。在专业领域中，教师 LLM 会在中间步骤出错，安全约束限制云端蒸馏，条件又不断变化，因此自进化成为现实路径。但朴素自进化会崩溃：仅用结果奖励使模型利用分布捷径，弱自评估会把伪路径固化为稳定失败模式。

Method: 利用关键不对称性——生成正确链条难，但验证单步容易。许多高价值领域存在确定性的可查询 oracle（物理模拟器或规则引擎），它能检查被断言的步骤而不具备教师级能力，且仅在规则范围内作答。基于此提出 CoEvo：单个模型交替扮演 Proposer 与 Solver。作为 Solver，模型生成多条竞争链，组内辩论暴露分歧步骤（能力边界的代理），由 oracle 裁决为过程级监督；作为 Proposer，同一模型在 oracle 约束内构造逐步更难的情景，将课程导向深度多跳链。两个角色联合更新，使训练压力与模型共同进化。

Result: 在工业、临床、法律的多步因果推理基准上，CoEvo 使 8B LLM 能够维持自进化，在路径正确率上超越蒸馏基线与最强专有参考（82.1% vs. 71.4%），且训练后的模型可泛化到未见类别和系统，保持根因准确率。

Conclusion: 通过 oracle 提供的过程级监督与共同进化的角色训练，CoEvo 让模型摆脱对教师蒸馏和结果奖励的依赖，实现可持续的自我进化，并在专业领域多步因果推理上取得优于基线的路径正确率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-step causal reasoning requires chaining inferences where each step constrains the next. An early error propagates silently, and a correct answer reached via flawed logic evades outcome-level detection. In specialized domains, teacher LLMs err on intermediate steps, safety constraints restrict cloud distillation, and shifting conditions demand adaptation, leaving self-evolution as the practical route. Naive self-evolution can collapse: outcome-only rewards let the model exploit distributional shortcuts, and weak self-evaluation reinforces spurious paths into stable failure patterns. We exploit a key asymmetry: generating a correct chain is hard, but verifying a single step is easy. Many high-stakes domains admit a deterministic, queryable oracle, a physics simulator or rule engine over codified constraints. It checks asserted steps without teacher-level ability and abstains beyond its rules; it can check what the model asserts, never replace it. This enables CoEvo, an oracle-grounded self-evolution framework where a single model alternates between Proposer and Solver. As Solver, the model generates competing chains; intra-group debate exposes disagreement steps, a proxy for the capability boundary, and the oracle adjudicates them into process-level supervision. As Proposer, the same model constructs progressively harder scenarios inside oracle constraints, steering the curriculum toward deep multi-hop chains. Both roles are updated jointly, so training pressure co-evolves with the model. On industrial, clinical, and legal multi-step causal reasoning benchmarks, CoEvo enables an 8B LLM to sustain self-evolution, surpassing distillation baselines and the strongest proprietary reference on path correctness (82.1% vs. 71.4%). The trained model generalizes to unseen categories and systems, preserving root-cause accuracy.

</details>


### [391] [Certified Mechanistic Interpretability: Lifting Single-Input Findings to Bounded Neighbourhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26112)
*Zhen Zhang,Yanliang Huang,Peng Xie,Wenyuan Wu,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于约束多项式zonotope（CPZ）传播的框架，将机械可解释性从单一输入观测提升为有界扰动邻域内的可证明陈述，并在注意力机制上实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性目前通过单输入逆向来刻画Transformer电路，所得机制缺乏对输入邻域的有界保证，导致单输入检验无法解决的不稳定性问题。

Method: 采用约束多项式zonotope（CPZ）传播框架；将三个内部注意力查询（top-k稳定性、证据质量、注意力熵）形式化为单纯形上的可解程序；证明CPZ传播精确保持softmax单纯形与LayerNorm零均值恒等式；用递归Jacobian zonotope构造跨层深度扩展证书，避免逐层生成元增长。

Result: 在Transformer注意力上实例化该框架，得到能够在有界扰动集上成立的可证明证书，补充单输入检查无法分辨的机械性陈述。

Conclusion: 该框架为机械可解释性提供形式化保证，可锐化机制性结论，并在经验启发式可能失效的场景中支持下游决策。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability reverse-engineers transformer circuits one input at a time, leaving observed mechanisms without guarantees over bounded input neighbourhoods. We address this gap with a framework based on constrained polynomial-zonotope (CPZ) propagation that lifts mechanistic-interpretability observations from a single input to certified statements over a bounded set of perturbations. Three internal-attention queries (top-$k$ stability, evidence mass, and attention entropy) are formulated as tractable programs over the simplex of attention weights, and CPZ propagation through transformer blocks is shown to preserve the softmax simplex and the LayerNorm zero-mean identity exactly. A recursive Jacobian zonotope construction extends the same certificates across layer depth by linearising the block stack at the input and avoids per-layer generator growth. We instantiate the framework on transformer attention; the resulting certificates offer a way to sharpen mechanistic statements that single-input inspection cannot resolve on its own, and to inform downstream decisions in regimes where empirical heuristics may be misleading.

</details>


### [392] [From Risk Scoring to Risk Allocation: A Density-Driven Framework for Diverse Monitoring in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26146)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文主张将多智能体风险监控从"风险打分"范式转变为"风险分配"范式：指出密度而非异常分数才是有效风险信号（Crowding Paradox），并将监控建模为带风险-多样性权衡的QUBO子集选择问题，取得显著效果并可在量子处理器上直接执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统风险监控基于逐状态打分并取Top-K的原始方法。在"拥挤"情形下（大量智能体共享相同脆弱性），这些告警的风险彼此相关，会产生冗余告警，即"监控中的羊群效应"。因此需要新的范式来避免冗余、实现多样化的风险分配。

Method: 1) 提出Crowding Paradox：P(risk|x) ∝ p(x) 而非 1/p(x)，说明密度而非异常分数才是有效风险信号；2) 基于密度得到脆弱性分数后，将监控问题重构为相互依赖状态上的组合子集选择，映射为带λ控制风险-多样性权衡的QUBO目标；3) 使用学习的λ策略逼近oracle网格搜索。

Result: 密度打分在金融数据上5d/10d/20d崩盘预测达到AUROC≥0.94，而五个异常基线均低于0.80；相对贪心算法的增益随规模单调上升（n=15时+24%，n=200时+66%）；学习λ策略达到oracle网格搜索目标的99.5%；该表述可迁移到交通与多智能体强化学习；相同QUBO实例无需修改即可在Rigetti超导QPUs（Ankaa-3、Cepheus-1-108Q，经Amazon Braket）上执行。

Conclusion: 监控应从风险打分转向风险分配。基于密度的脆弱性打分配合QUBO子集选择，其Pareto前沿包含MMR、k-DPP等标准多样化子集方法作为固定工作点，且优于贪心方法。量子处理器上的可执行性仅作为该表述的兼容性属性报告，并不声称在此规模上具有量子优势。

Abstract: Risk monitoring in multi-agent systems is commonly built on a per-state primitive that scores each state independently and selects the top K. Under crowding, where many agents share the same fragility, this approach picks redundant alerts whose risks are jointly correlated, a pattern we describe as ``herding in monitoring.'' We propose a paradigm shift from risk scoring to risk allocation, supported by two contributions. First, we identify the Crowding Paradox, namely that P(risk | x) $\propto$ p(x) rather than 1/p(x), so density rather than anomaly score is the operative risk signal; on financial data, density-based scoring reaches AUROC $\geq$ 0.94 at 5d/10d/20d crash horizons, while five anomaly baselines all fall below 0.80. Second, given a density-derived fragility score, we recast monitoring as combinatorial subset selection over interdependent states and map it to a QUBO objective with a $λ$-controlled risk--diversity tradeoff. The resulting Pareto frontier contains standard diverse-subset methods (MMR, k-DPP) as fixed operating points; the gain over greedy grows monotonically with scale, from +24% at n=15 to +66% at n=200; a learned $λ$ policy reaches 99.5% of an oracle grid-search objective; and the formulation transfers to traffic and multi-agent reinforcement learning. The same QUBO instances execute without modification on Rigetti superconducting QPUs (Ankaa-3 and Cepheus-1-108Q via Amazon Braket), which we report as a compatibility property of the formulation rather than a claim of quantum advantage at this scale.

</details>


### [393] [Block-Level Weight-Space Structure Persists Under Post-Training: An Empirical Study Across LLM Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26147)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文实证研究后训练如何改变权重空间几何，发现后训练是"结构化扰动"：改变每个张量但保留块级结构，并据此构建LinkerLLM实现显存节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM以共享预训练权重的后训练变体家族形式部署。作者希望理解后训练在权重空间几何层面究竟做了什么，并探索这一性质能否用于实际的内存优化。

Method: 对四个架构家族（Qwen2.5、Llama-3.1/3.2、Mistral、Gemma-2）共八种配置进行实证分析，计算张量级字节一致性与块级余弦相似度、相对Frobenius距离；并构建LinkerLLM惰性加载器，在共驻变体间共享块。

Result: 存在"粒度差距"：291-339个张量中无一字节相同（去重0%收益），但块级平均余弦相似度超0.99、相对Frobenius距离低于0.13。独立训练的专用模型（如Qwen2.5-Coder）与通用基座相似度约0.64。LinkerLLM实现18-48%显存节省，可在单块24GB消费级GPU上运行多达五个7B变体；八种配置中五种保留至少94%质量，另三种各有1个基准处于87-91%。

Conclusion: 后训练对权重空间施加的是结构化扰动，改变每个参数但保持块级几何；该性质不普遍适用于独立训练的专用模型。基于此可利用块共享显著降低部署显存开销。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are deployed as families of post-trained variants (base, instruct, chat, code) derived from a shared set of pre-trained weights. We present an empirical study of how post-training transforms weight-space geometry, covering eight configurations across four architecture families (Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1/3.2, Mistral, Gemma-2). We identify a granularity gap: post-training modifies every tensor (zero of 291-339 tensors remain byte-identical, so hash-based deduplication achieves 0% savings), yet preserves block-level structure (mean cosine similarity exceeds 0.99 and relative Frobenius distance stays below 0.13). Post-training therefore acts as a structured perturbation that shifts every parameter while leaving block-level geometry intact. The property is not universal: independently trained specializations (for example, Qwen2.5-Coder) attain cosine similarity around 0.64 with the general base, indicating a disconnected region of weight space. Perturbation magnitude varies systematically with model scale, architecture family, and post-training recipe. As a practical application, we build LinkerLLM, a lazy loader that aliases shareable blocks across co-resident variants, achieving 18-48% GPU memory savings and enabling up to five 7B-parameter variants on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. Five of eight configurations retain at least 94% of the unshared variant's quality on MMLU, ARC-Challenge, HellaSwag, and WinoGrande; the remaining three (Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-2B, Llama-3.2-1B) have one below-threshold benchmark each (87-91%), which we report transparently rather than gate the block-sharing decision on a single threshold.

</details>


### [394] [Spectral Tail Interventions in Decoder-Only Language Models: Reasoning-Sensitive Weight Structure from Controlled Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26165)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Sanjida Akhter,Md Najmus Swaqeeb,Abu Sa-Adat Mohamed Moon-Im Al Ahsan,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过受控干预研究解码器-only Transformer中权重空间上谱尾部与语言模型行为的计算关联，发现学习到的尾部编辑比频谱匹配对照更具破坏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间结构与语言模型行为常相关，但仅相关性不能确立计算层面的参与关系，需要受控干预加以验证。

Method: 在固定相对偏移下推导有限宽度条件界，将平方奇异值的逆参与率与中心pre-softmax logit峰度联系；定义逐点query–key乘积尾部目标，比较独立因子手术与保留原生注意力计算的产品目标分解；采用五个固定频谱匹配的Haar对照，并在三个基础检查点、五个推理基准及一个指令微调Phi检查点上评估，进行Holm校正，隔离QK、value–output和MLP组件，并开展逆参与率与准确率转变、残差化尾部感知LoRA的独立研究。

Result: 在全部20个模型–任务单元中，学习到的尾部编辑比五个固定频谱匹配Haar对照的均值更具破坏性；18个配对对比经Holm校正后仍显著，2个具有方向性但不结论性；产品目标因子获得更高的留出尾部子空间比例；逆参与率在匹配跨越规则下先于汇总准确率转变；残差化尾部感知LoRA比标准LoRA和PiSSA更早达到目标，但最终分数区间重叠。

Conclusion: 结论仅限于所评估的检查点、层、任务、干预和对照，表明上谱尾部与模型行为存在计算层面的关联证据。

Abstract: Weight-space structure often correlates with language-model behavior, but correlation alone does not establish computational involvement. We study concentrated upper spectral tails in decoder-only transformers through controlled interventions. At a fixed relative offset, we derive a finite-width conditional bound linking the inverse participation ratio of squared singular values to central pre-softmax logit kurtosis. We then define a pointwise query--key ($QK$) product-tail target and compare independent factor surgery with a product-targeted factorization that preserves native attention computation. Across three base checkpoints and five reasoning benchmarks, plus an instruction-tuned Phi checkpoint analyzed separately, the learned-tail edit is more damaging than the mean of five fixed spectrum-matched Haar controls in all 20 model--task cells. Eighteen paired contrasts remain significant after Holm correction, while two are directional but inconclusive. Product-targeted factors attain higher held-out tail-subspace fractions, providing an empirical bridge between product- and factor-level interventions. Component isolation identifies contributions from $QK$, value--output, and multilayer-perceptron blocks, although the theorem covers only $QK$. In separate studies, inverse participation precedes pooled accuracy transitions under a matched crossing rule, and residualized tail-aware low-rank adaptation (LoRA) reaches targets earlier than standard LoRA and PiSSA while final-score intervals overlap. Conclusions are restricted to the evaluated checkpoints, layers, tasks, interventions, and controls.

</details>


### [395] [Activation-Energy Pruning for Spiking Neural Networks: Unsupervised Personalization via Spike-Count Saliency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26167)
*Joseph Bingham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将激活能剪枝（activation-energy pruning）应用于脉冲神经网络（SNN），发现梯度类剪枝方法在SNN上显著失效，而激活能剪枝在高稀疏度下反而能提升性能，暗示其与皮层经验依赖特化的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活能剪枝此前已被证明是传统深度神经网络的有效无监督个性化策略。在SNN中，激活能不仅是启发式指标，而是与每个突触代谢成本成正比的物理量。作者希望探究：当同一剪枝准则应用于SNN时会发生什么。

Method: 将激活能剪枝（移除幅度与累积突触前脉冲计数乘积低于阈值的权重）应用于脉冲神经网络，并与基于梯度的剪枝方法（SNIP、GraSP、幅度剪枝）在多种架构和数据集上进行对比，包括在神经形态基准（如N-MNIST）上测试高稀疏度表现。

Result: 1) 梯度类剪枝方法在SNN上持续表现不佳，在σ=0.2稀疏度时准确率就接近随机水平，作者归因于代理梯度显著性估计与二值脉冲序列表示之间的系统性不兼容（但不排除代理选择或超参数可部分缓解）。2) 激活能剪枝在神经形态基准上于高稀疏度下反而超过源模型（σ=0.8时N-MNIST为98.4±0.4% vs 97.2±0.7%）。

Conclusion: 激活能剪枝在SNN中表现出与传统网络截然不同的特性：梯度类方法失效，而激活能剪枝在高稀疏度下可提升性能。作者将此解释为与经验依赖的皮层特化相一致——移除仅对非目标类别活跃的连接可减少跨类干扰，产生更干净的目标表示，但强调这仅为解释性类比而非机制性证明。

Abstract: Activation-energy pruning -- removing weights whose product of magnitude and cumulative pre-synaptic spike count falls below a threshold -- was established as an effective unsupervised personalization strategy for conventional deep neural networks~\citep{BINGHAM2025101242}. This paper asks what happens when the same criterion is applied to spiking neural networks (SNNs), where activation energy is not merely a useful heuristic but a literal physical quantity proportional to the metabolic cost of each synapse. The answer is surprising on three counts.
  First, gradient-based pruning methods that perform competitively on conventional networks (SNIP, GraSP, magnitude pruning) consistently underperform on SNNs, collapsing to near-chance accuracy by $σ= 0.2$ sparsity across all tested architectures and datasets. We trace this to a systematic incompatibility between surrogate-gradient saliency estimation and the binary spike-train representation, though we cannot rule out that alternative surrogate choices or hyperparameter settings might partially mitigate the effect.
  Second, activation-energy pruning applied to a neuromorphic benchmark \emph{improves} over the source model at high sparsity ($98.4 \pm 0.4\%$ vs.\ $97.2 \pm 0.7\%$ at $σ= 0.8$ on N-MNIST), a phenomenon with no counterpart in the conventional network setting. We interpret this result as consistent with experience-dependent cortical specialisation: removing connections active only for non-target classes may reduce cross-class interference and produce a cleaner target representation, though we note this is an interpretive analogy rather than a mechanistic demonstration.

</details>


### [396] [Component Type, Not Reconstruction Error, Predicts Attention Quantization Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26173)
*Kasun Dewage,Marianna Pensky,Suranadi De Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在9个开放权重模型上系统性量化单个注意力投影后发现，权重重构误差对困惑度敏感性的预测力很弱（中位R²=0.044），组件类型与层身份更具解释力，V投影常为主导敏感组件，故仅凭重构误差不足以支撑敏感度感知的比特分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练后量化（PTQ）方法多依赖逐层重构、二阶代理目标或激活感知变换来减小量化误差，但这些误差信号是否能预测量化单个注意力投影对下游功能的实际影响，尚未被直接刻画。作者希望厘清重构误差与实际敏感性之间的关系，以指导混合精度比特分配。

Method: 扫描9个开放权重语言模型（1.3B–8B；OPT、GPT-J、LLaMA-1/2/3、Mistral、Qwen 2.5），每次仅量化一个注意力投影，在RTN（全部9个模型）及GPTQ（7个模型）下采用3比特与4比特，记录重构误差、困惑度变化以及逐投影的激活加权量化误差，共获得3,808个独立测量。通过方差解释（R²）对比重构误差、组件类型（Q/K/V/O）与层身份对困惑度敏感性的预测能力。

Result: （1）在给定组件类型内，RTN下36个案例中有27个重构误差对困惑度敏感性方差的解释不足10%，R²中位数为0.044；（2）在全部9个模型中，组件类型与层身份均比重构误差解释更多方差，其中层身份在7个模型中是最强预测因子，组件类型在其余2个中最强；（3）V投影是最常见的主导组件，在9个模型中的7个里贡献了总正向ΔPPL的38–51%；（4）主导组件在RTN与GPTQ之间大体保持一致（7例中5例）；（5）激活加权量化误差对V投影的组件内预测力中等优于重构误差（R²中位数0.20 vs. 0.06）。

Conclusion: 仅凭相对权重重构误差不足以进行敏感性感知的比特分配；组件类型、尤其是层身份是更强的预测因子，且V投影在混合精度方案中值得专门考虑。

Abstract: Many post-training quantization (PTQ) methods use layer-wise reconstruction, second-order proxy objectives, or activation-aware transformations to reduce quantization-induced error. Whether that error signal predicts the downstream functional impact of quantizing an individual attention projection has not been directly characterized. We sweep nine open-weight language models (1.3B--8B parameters; OPT, GPT-J, LLaMA-1/2/3, Mistral, Qwen 2.5) and quantize one attention projection at a time under round-to-nearest (RTN) and, for seven models, GPTQ at 3 and 4 bits, recording reconstruction error, perplexity change, and per-projection activation-weighted quantization error for 3,808 distinct measurements. We find: (1) within a given component type (Q, K, V, or O), reconstruction error explains less than 10% of the variance in perplexity sensitivity in 27 of 36 cases under RTN, with median R^2 = 0.044; (2) both component type and layer identity explain more variance than reconstruction error in all 9 models, with layer identity the strongest predictor in 7 of 9 models and component type strongest in the remaining 2; (3) value (V) projections are the most commonly dominant component, accounting for 38--51% of total positive Delta PPL in seven of nine models; (4) the dominant component is broadly preserved between RTN and GPTQ (5 of 7 cases); and (5) activation-weighted quantization error is a moderately better within-component predictor than reconstruction error for V projections specifically (median R^2 of 0.20 vs. 0.06). These findings indicate that relative weight reconstruction error alone is insufficient for sensitivity-aware bit allocation, and that V projections merit dedicated consideration in mixed-precision schemes.

</details>


### [397] [Partially Observed Sparse Graphs: The Unknown Sampling Rate is a Tail Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26199)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在稀疏可交换图（graphex）模型下，非孤立节点比例满足 n_s/n_1→s^{1+σ}，从而未知采样率可由尾指数 σ 推断，全局边数估计由此等价于尾指数估计；代入闭合式尾指数估计器后无需拟合即可达到约 21.7% 的中位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型图通常只能获得部分采样（如预算受限的爬取、面板数据、部分转储）。当采样比例 s 已知时，边数估计 e_s/s² 无需模型；但实际中 s 常未知而总体规模已知，此时缺乏可靠估计方法。现有二次图模型（graphon）估计器 σ=0 的情形在稀疏图上失效，且并非拟合问题而是恒等式问题。

Method: 在稀疏可交换图（graphex）模型下推导期望非孤立比例关系 n_s/n_1→s^{1+σ}；据此将未知采样率表示为尾指数 σ 的函数并回代，得到估计量 e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}；对替换的有限规模误差给出界，并证明该归约对尾指数估计器是模块化的；用已发表的闭合式尾指数估计器进行验证，对比二次图模型估计器作为 σ=0 特例。

Result: 边基数估计在期望意义上即尾指数估计；二次估计器 σ=0 为特例，其失败源于恒等式而非拟合（中位误差 260% 对 27%）。模块化归约配闭合式估计器在 13 个网络、39 个采样预算上达到 21.7% 误差且无需拟合。拟合完整 graphex 还能返回任意规模下的度分布与生成对象，稀疏性作为一个坐标。两个精确极限：秩一 graphex 的传递性由度分布固定，故高聚类图不属于该类；在滚雪球或随机游走爬取下所有方法均失效，基于设计的 oracle 最差（7.8%–588%）。

Conclusion: 在 s 未知、总体规模已知的稀疏图边数估计问题中，所提归约将问题转化为尾指数估计，提供了模块化、无需拟合的估计框架，并揭示了二次图模型估计器失效的本质；同时明确了两类失效边界（高聚类图与滚雪球/随机游走采样）。

Abstract: A large graph is often available only in part: a crawl stopped by its budget, a panel, a partial dump. When the sampled fraction $s$ is known by design the total edge count follows from $\hat e=e_s/s^2$ and no model is needed. We treat the case where $s$ is unknown and the population size is known. Our main result is a reduction: under a sparse exchangeable (graphex) model the expected non-isolated fraction obeys $n_s/n_1\to s^{1+σ}$, so the sampling rate becomes estimable once the tail index $σ$ is, and substituting it back gives $e_s(n_1/n_s)^{2/(1+σ)}$ -- the same estimator, with the design quantity inferred. Estimating global edge cardinality in a sparse graph is therefore, in expectation, tail-index estimation, and the quadratic graphon estimator is the case $σ=0$: it fails by an identity rather than by a fit ($260\%$ median error against $27\%$). We bound the finite-size error of the substitution and show the reduction is \emph{modular} in the tail-index estimator --- filled with a published closed-form one it reaches $21.7\%$ over $13$ networks and $39$ sampling budgets with no fitting at all. Fitting a full graphex additionally returns the degree distribution at any size and a generative object, in a representation where sparsity is a coordinate and the interpolation path is dictated rather than chosen. Two limits are exact: rank-one graphexes have transitivity fixed by the degree profile, so high-clustering graphs lie outside the class; and under snowball or random-walk crawls every method here fails, the design-based oracle worst of all ($7.8\%$ to $588\%$).

</details>


### [398] [Bridging the Data Gap: Digital Twin as a New Paradigm for AI-based Radio Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26214)
*Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Ali Al Khansa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以环境3D数字孪生为核心的大规模无线电感知方法：用DT作为世界模型，通过射线追踪生成大量合成CIR时序，训练序列神经网络实现无设备定位（DFL），并主张将感知视为环境条件化的学习问题，训练特定场地的局部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线电感知（尤其无设备定位）通常依赖单一全局模型，难以在不同环境、布局和动态变化下大规模部署；实际采集标注数据成本高、难以覆盖多样场景，因此需要一种可扩展、可条件化的感知范式。

Method: 构建环境3D数字孪生，提供几何、材质与收发机位置，驱动射线追踪引擎生成时间索引的信道冲激响应（CIR），覆盖移动人员/物体、布局变化、季节与天气等多种合理场景；用这些合成序列训练序列神经网络，将CIR时序映射为空间占用估计，实现无设备定位。提出将感知视为环境条件化学习问题，训练或微调专属某场地DT的局部模型。首个实验引入一种新的状态空间模型（SSM）架构，在多个房间几何中训练与评估。

Result: 在多个房间几何上完成训练与评估，所获得的无设备定位性能验证了该方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 以3D数字孪生为世界模型、生成合成CIR数据并训练环境条件化局部模型，是推动大规模无线电感知与无设备定位的可行路径；新SSM架构的初步定位结果表明该思路具有应用前景。

Abstract: We present a methodology that places a 3D digital twin (DT) of the environment as the main enabler behind the development of radio sensing at scale. The DT acts as a world model, providing geometry, materials, and transmitter/receiver placements to a ray-tracing engine that generates time-indexed channel impulse responses (CIRs) for large numbers of plausible scenes (moving people and objects, layout variants, seasonal/weather conditions, etc). From these synthetic sequences, we train a sequential neural network that maps CIR time series to spatial occupancy estimates, enabling device-free localization (DFL) without instrumented targets. We posit that sensing is best approached as an environment-conditioned learning problem: rather than seeking a single global model, we advocate training or fine-tuning local models specialized to a site-specific DT. As a first experiment, we introduce a novel State Space Model architecture, trained and evaluated across multiple room geometries. The localization performances obtained demonstrate the potential of the approach.

</details>


### [399] [Beyond Imitation: Auditing the Recoverability of Reasoning in Distilled Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26216)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出"前缀恢复"指标衡量学生能否在给定正确解法前缀后独立完成推理，并将其与交叉熵和反向KL梯度间的余弦冲突联系起来，发现分布匹配蒸馏的收益在学生容量较低、正确轨迹恢复不均匀时最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师给出的正确解只有在接收学生能够继续推理时才算有用监督。作者希望量化这种"兼容性"，并解释为何分布级蒸馏（反向KL）在不同规模学生上效果差异大，从而为选择高成本蒸馏方法提供实用诊断依据。

Method: 提出前缀恢复度量：揭示已验证解法的25%、50%或75%前缀后，测试学生能否正确完成剩余推理。将恢复能力与全词表上交叉熵和反向KL梯度的余弦冲突相关联。在Qwen3相邻师生对（0.6B至8B）上实验，并在固定1000条轨迹队列上统计恢复率与鲁棒-脆弱恢复差距；另进行独立目标干预实验。

Result: 反向KL蒸馏在2B以下学生上带来最一致的数学和代码提升。学生规模从0.6B增至4B时，平均前缀恢复由71.0%升至91.9%，鲁棒-脆弱恢复差距从46.0个百分点收窄至14.4个百分点。教师固定为8B时，冲突分离度从0.993降至0.233。独立目标干预在脆弱轨迹上显示出最大的反向KL救援效果。

Conclusion: 三项测量共同指向一个容量依赖的迁移机制：当正确轨迹恢复不均匀时，分布匹配的改进空间最大。前缀恢复可作为选择昂贵分布级蒸馏方法的实用诊断工具。

Abstract: A correct teacher solution becomes useful supervision when the receiving student can continue its reasoning. We measure this compatibility with prefix recovery: after revealing 25%, 50%, or 75% of a verified solution, we test whether the student completes it correctly. We connect recovery to the cosine conflict between cross-entropy and reverse-KL gradients over the full vocabulary. Across adjacent Qwen3 teacher-student pairs from 0.6B to 8B parameters, reverse-KL distillation delivers its most consistent mathematical and code improvements for the two students below 2B parameters. On a fixed cohort of 1,000 trajectories, average prefix recovery rises from 71.0% to 91.9% as student size increases from 0.6B to 4B, and the robust-fragile recovery gap contracts from 46.0 to 14.4 percentage points. With the teacher fixed at 8B, conflict separation falls from 0.993 to 0.233. An independent objective intervention finds the largest reverse-KL rescue on fragile trajectories. The three measurements locate the same capacity-dependent transfer regime: distribution matching has the greatest headroom when correct traces remain unevenly recoverable. Prefix recovery provides a practical diagnostic for selecting costly distribution-level distillation.

</details>


### [400] [MSA-CITE: A Co-Adapted LoRA Specialist Ecology for Fixed-Budget Small-Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26217)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将多个被丢弃的后训练检查点（LoRA分支）视为可组合的部署资产，通过多路径采样与校准先验选择答案，在数学任务上优于最强单分支基线，但改进的稳健性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑语言模型通常只保留单一后训练检查点并重复采样，浪费了多个后训练轨迹中蕴含的多样性。

Method: 从Qwen3-4B骨干出发保留四个冻结LoRA分支，每个分支采样一次（固定四次生成预算）；将答案按等价类分组，用校准得到的来源先验求和打分，并以确定性规则选代表，不学习、不额外生成或验证。

Result: 200道数学题上达65.5%准确率（最强单分支62.0%）；100项学科错位偏移下42.0%对40.0%；同分布条件下优于同质SFT和Online-OPD重复，但对最强基线及偏移条件下结果不具决定性。

Conclusion: 后训练分支即使未共同训练，也能在部署中产生集体收益，这是一个范围有限但具体的贡献。

Abstract: Compact language models are typically deployed by retaining a single post-training checkpoint and sampling it repeatedly. In this work, we challenge this practice by treating multiple discarded checkpoints as composable assets for deployment. Starting from a single Qwen3-4B backbone, we preserve four frozen LoRA branches, each derived from a different post-training trajectory. stead of drawing four generations from one branch, we allocate a fixed four-generation budget by sampling one completion from each branch. Our method, Multi-path Specialist Adaptation with Calibrated Inference-Time Evidence (MSA-CITE), processes the resulting portfolio by grouping terminal answers into equivalence classes, scoring each class via summed calibration-derived source priors, and selecting a representative under deterministic tie-breaking rules. The readout stage does not learn from evaluation results, nor does it introduce additional generations, verifiers, or reranking steps. On 200 held-out mathematics items, the four-path portfolio achieves 65.5% accuracy, compared with 62.0% for the strongest single-branch baseline. On a 100-item subject-disjoint shift, it attains 42.0% versus 40.0%. Under in-distribution conditions, the improvements over homogeneous SFT and Online-OPD repetition are robust; results against the strongest baseline and under shifted conditions are not conclusive. Our findings offer a narrow but concrete contribution: post-training branches, even without co-training, can be collectively beneficial for deployment.

</details>


### [401] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Modeling for Continuous and Subtle GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26231)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出高阶液体证据检测器，将微弱GNSS欺骗检测建模为因果序贯证据建模问题，通过运动一致性残差与高阶证据交互估计因果欺骗概率，在AV-GPS数据集上取得AUROC 0.9932。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续微弱的GNSS欺骗对自动驾驶汽车威胁严重：伪造位置局部看似合理，却逐渐与非GNSS车载传感器观测的运动不一致。现有面向AV的检测器多依赖残差阈值或特征级分类，对微弱GNSS-运动不一致如何随时间发展并持续缺乏建模。

Method: 方法分三步：1）比较连续GNSS位置隐含位移与独立车载运动观测推断位移之差，转换为不确定性归一化残差证据；2）将当前不一致、局部演化、超常水平、累积持续性和位移有效性表示为因果弱证据，映射到瞬时、演化和持续潜状态，经有界Kirchhoff式对称交换对齐，通过显式三阶交互组合；3）用二阶液体动力学追踪其记忆与演化，估计因果欺骗概率，再经验证集选择的阈值与持续性参数转为确认告警。

Result: 在AV-GPS数据集族上实验，展现出强受控与外部泛化能力及清晰的序贯告警行为。Dataset-1上AUROC为0.9932、AUPRC为0.9843，并在基于学习的基线中获得最低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 将微弱GNSS欺骗检测形式化为因果序贯证据建模问题，并用高阶液体证据检测器有效捕捉证据随时间的协调演化，可提升检测精度并降低误报。

Abstract: Continuous and subtle GNSS spoofing poses a serious threat to autonomous vehicles because forged positions may remain locally plausible while gradually becoming inconsistent with vehicle motion observed by non-GNSS onboard sensors. Existing AV-oriented detectors commonly rely on residual thresholds or feature-level classification and provide limited modeling of how weak GNSS--motion inconsistency develops and persists over time. This paper formulates subtle GNSS spoofing detection as a causal sequential evidence-modeling problem and proposes a high-order liquid evidence detector. The method first compares the displacement implied by consecutive GNSS positions with that inferred from independent onboard motion observations and converts their difference into uncertainty-normalized residual evidence. It then represents the current inconsistency, its local evolution, excess above the normal level, accumulated persistence, and displacement validity as causal weak evidence. These cues are mapped into instantaneous, evolutionary, and persistent latent states, aligned through a bounded Kirchhoff-inspired symmetric exchange, and combined through an explicit third-order interaction to capture their coordinated support for spoofing. To model how this coordinated evidence develops over time, second-order liquid dynamics track its memory and evolution to estimate causal spoofing probabilities, which are converted into confirmed alarms using validation-selected threshold and persistence parameters. Experiments on the AV--GPS dataset family demonstrate strong controlled and external generalization, together with clear sequential alarm behavior. On Dataset-1, the proposed detector achieves an AUROC of 0.9932 and an AUPRC of 0.9843, while obtaining the lowest false-positive rate among the learning-based baselines. Code: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/HO-LLN-Spoofing.

</details>


### [402] [Can You Delete a Year of Market Data? Machine Unlearning Against Exact Retraining Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26242)
*Junyi Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对时间序列预测模型的机器遗忘进行基准测试，以全量训练的参考模型和无该时段数据的重训练预言机为对照，发现2020年疫情年记忆差距最大，且近似遗忘应以预言机而非低预测精度为目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据许可到期后，仅删除存储记录无法消除已训练预测模型中编码的影响，而重训练成本高，因此需要研究无需重训练的机器遗忘方法。现有工作缺乏针对时间序列预测、尤其是金融波动率面板的系统基准与评估标准。

Method: 在S&P 500波动率面板上构建3,200对配对参照（全量数据训练的参考模型与剔除指定时段重训练的预言机），网格覆盖5种架构、4个滚动折、5个可删除年份和3种实验性删除水平；比较十二对可部署架构-方法组合，并分析审计分离度、窗口级损失比较及股票级窗口独立性假设的影响。

Result: 2020年COVID危机年对所有架构产生最大记忆差距，删除该年使三个可部署模型在每一折均改善，2022年熊市中提升最大，两个非可部署模型表现不一致；某Transformer单元中，从未训练2020年的预言机仍以0.51信息系数预测该年（参考为0.55），将预测推向噪声会降低测试技能；十二对可部署组合中仅TSMixer结合hinge方法在每一折接近预言机，弥补74-118%的参考-预言机差距且无明显测试技能损失；方法排名随架构和滚动窗口变化；审计分离度随先前记忆升高但精确删除后仍可能很小；窗口级损失比较最高为0.69，将股票级窗口视为独立会使绝对t统计量中位数膨胀1.9倍。

Conclusion: 研究呼吁明确删除范围、针对相关架构与窗口进行预言机验证，并采用考虑统计功效的审计方法；近似遗忘的目标应是预言机而非删除时段上的低预测精度。

Abstract: When a data license expires, deleting stored records does not remove influence encoded in a trained forecaster. Machine unlearning seeks to remove this influence without retraining. We benchmark temporal unlearning with 3,200 paired references trained on all data and oracles retrained without the requested period. The grid covers five architectures, four rolling folds, five deletable years, and three experimental deletion levels on an S&P 500 volatility panel. The 2020 COVID crisis year produces the largest memorization gap for every architecture. Removing it improves all three deployable models in every fold, with the largest improvement in the 2022 bear market, while the two non-deployable models respond inconsistently. The target for approximate unlearning is the oracle, not low predictive accuracy on the deleted period. In one Transformer cell, an oracle that never trained on 2020 still predicts it at an information coefficient of 0.51, compared with 0.55 for the reference; pushing predictions toward noise reduces test skill. Across twelve deployable architecture-method pairs, only TSMixer with the hinge method remains near the oracle in every fold, closing 74-118% of the reference-to-oracle gap without a measurable loss of test skill. Method rankings vary across architectures and rolling windows. Audit separation rises with prior memorization but can remain small after exact deletion. The window-level loss comparison reaches at most 0.69, and treating stock-level windows as independent inflates the absolute t-statistic by a median factor of 1.9. These results call for an explicit deletion scope, oracle validation for the relevant architecture and window, and power-aware auditing.

</details>


### [403] [Information-Theoretic Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26257)
*Yunfei Zhang,Wen Wen,Tieliang Gong,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPT4CL方法，将CLIP文本提示解耦为任务共享提示分布和类特定提示，解决持续学习中的检索依赖与分类器偏置问题，并给出理论泛化界，实验取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习（CL）希望在顺序数据上增量获取知识并避免灾难性遗忘。近期提示微调成为将预训练模型适配到CL任务的高效手段，但现有提示设计范式普遍存在两个问题：一是检索依赖（retrieval dependence），使模型适配对提示选择敏感；二是分类器偏置（classifier bias），导致预测偏向新到达的类别。

Method: 提出Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning（DPT4CL）。该方法将CLIP的文本提示解耦为两部分：一是任务共享提示分布，通过优化信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）目标得到，以促进跨任务知识迁移并缓解分类器偏置；二是类特定提示，用于增强类间可分性，且不依赖显式的提示检索。此外，从信息论角度建立了统一的超额风险（excess risk）界，为框架的鲁棒泛化与遗忘缓解提供理论支撑。

Result: 在标准持续学习基准上的大量实验表明，DPT4CL达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）的性能。源代码已开源。

Conclusion: DPT4CL通过解耦提示设计有效缓解了检索依赖和分类器偏置，兼顾了跨任务知识迁移与类间可分性，并有信息论理论保证其泛化性与遗忘抑制，在持续学习任务上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to incrementally acquire knowledge from sequential data while avoiding catastrophic forgetting. Recently, prompt tuning has attracted increasing attention as an efficient approach for adapting pre-trained models to CL tasks. However, existing prompt design paradigms commonly suffer from retrieval dependence and classifier bias, which make model adaptation sensitive to prompt selection and bias predictions toward newly arrived classes. To address these challenges, we propose Decoupled Prompt Tuning for Continual Learning (DPT4CL), which decouples the CLIP textual prompt into a task-shared prompt distribution and class-specific prompts. The task-shared prompt distribution is derived by optimizing an Information Bottleneck objective to facilitate cross-task knowledge transfer and alleviate classifier bias, while class-specific prompts enhance inter-class separability without relying on explicit prompt retrieval. Furthermore, we establish a unified excess risk bound from an information-theoretic perspective, providing theoretical support for the robust generalization and forgetting mitigation of the proposed framework. Extensive experiments on standard CL benchmarks demonstrate that DPT4CL achieves state-of-the-art performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/Cloudfly-Z/DPT4CL

</details>


### [404] [Mode Collapse Is Cheap to Detect: A Ground-Truth-Free Pre-Flight Check for Neural Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26272)
*Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"预检"诊断方法，用于判断无真值样本训练的神经采样器是否遗漏了目标分布的部分质量。该方法仅需能量及其一阶、二阶导数，以极小训练成本估计被遗漏质量，并在多种可精确计算真值的分布上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经采样器在无样本条件下针对未归一化目标训练，可能在不被察觉的情况下遗漏目标的一部分质量。常用的诊断指标（如自归一化有效样本量 ESS、ELBO）都基于模型自身采样，局限于模型支撑集内，无法发现遗漏的模态。作者举例：某采样器的自归一化 ESS 达 0.99，却遗漏了 87% 的目标质量。作者主张"检测"遗漏质量比"采样"它容易得多。

Method: 作者将检测问题转化为局部的曲率探测：检测每个被遗漏的盆地仅需一个点加上局部曲率估计，而纠正则需要重新训练采样器。据此设计了一个预检流程，消耗采样器自身训练预算的百分之几，只使用能量 E、梯度 ∇E 和海森 ∇²E。该方法还配有一个"自诊断"机制，在无真值时给出保守方向的判断。

Result: 在 Gaussian-mixture、Many-Well 及旋转各向异性 Many-Well 等具有可精确计算真值的目标上，该方法以 2.7% 的训练成本将遗漏质量估计误差控制在 10⁻³ 以内；相比之下，调优后的退火 SMC 参考方法需耗费 70%–280% 的训练成本且效果更差。该方法同样适用于无可处理密度的受控 SDE 采样器（此时 ESS 与 ELBO 均无法计算）。自诊断机制在 60 组配置中通过 16 组，其中 15 组精度达 10⁻² 或更好。

Conclusion: 该预检方法能够以低成本提供"存在遗漏质量"的证据，有时也能提供"搜索已稳定"的证据，但并不能认证一次训练的成功，其阈值也是启发式的。作者明确界定了方法的适用边界，并在真实物理势能面 LJ-13 上绘制了方法的失效边界，报告了失败位置及原因。

Abstract: Neural samplers are trained against an unnormalised target $\tildeπ=e^{-E}$ with no samples from $π$, which leaves the practitioner with no way to tell whether an expensive training run has silently dropped part of the target. The diagnostics in common use are computed from the model's own draws and are therefore confined to the model's support: we exhibit a sampler whose self-normalised effective sample size is $0.99$ while it misses $87\%$ of the target mass. We argue that \emph{detecting} missing mass is a strictly easier problem than sampling it: detection needs one point per missed basin plus a local curvature estimate, whereas correction needs the sampler retrained. We turn this into a pre-flight check that consumes a few percent of the sampler's own training budget and uses only $E$, $\nabla E$ and $\nabla^2 E$. On Gaussian-mixture, Many-Well and rotated anisotropic Many-Well targets with exactly computable ground truth, the check estimates the missing mass to within $10^{-3}$ at $2.7\%$ of training cost, where a tuned annealed SMC reference needs $70$--$280\%$ of training cost to do worse. It also applies unchanged to a controlled-SDE sampler that has no tractable density, where ESS and the ELBO cannot be formed at all. The estimator carries a \emph{self-diagnostic} that, without ground truth, is conservative in the safe direction: across $60$ configurations it clears $16$, of which $15$ are accurate to $10^{-2}$ or better. We are explicit about what this does and does not license: the check cheaply produces evidence of missing mass, and sometimes evidence that the search has stabilised, but it cannot certify a run, and its thresholds are heuristic. We then map the boundary of the method on a real physical landscape, LJ-13, and report where it fails and why.

</details>


### [405] [JAMPR+/L2D: scalable neural heuristic for constrained vehicle routing problems in dynamic environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26275)
*Andrew Soroka,Alex Meshcheryakov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型在CPDPTW问题上的迁移与泛化能力，通过在CVRPLIB和ORTEC数据上的实验，证明其在多数实例上优于HGS启发式方法，并提出快速微调策略应对距离矩阵的显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆路径问题（含容量限制、时间窗、取送货、多仓库等现实约束的CPDPTW）计算挑战大。传统精确与启发式方法在中小规模（N≲100）有效，但面对大规模物流任务时缺乏适应性和可扩展性。作者希望验证深度强化学习模型在大规模及距离矩阵变化场景下的实用性。

Method: 采用已有的JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型求解大规模CPDPTW问题，并将其应用于图距离矩阵发生显著变化的情形。在CVRPLIB基准上测试其对中等规模CVRP和VRPTW问题的性能，并考察其在约束简化、不同问题规模及距离矩阵中度变化下的泛化性；针对距离矩阵重大变化，提出对JAMPR+进行快速微调的策略。

Result: 在CVRPLIB基准上，JAMPR+/L2D在超过85%的实例中优于最先进的启发式方法HGS，并在目标差距上取得改进。在CPDPTW上训练的模型可良好泛化到约束更简单的CVRP、VRPTW，以及不同问题规模和距离矩阵的中度变化。在ORTEC数据（CPDPTW）上，快速微调策略在无需完整重训练的情况下显著降低了目标差距。

Conclusion: JAMPR+/L2D深度强化学习模型在CPDPTW及更简单路径问题上具有良好性能与泛化能力，配合快速微调策略可在距离矩阵变化的实际路径场景中兼顾精度与快速推理，为动态物流场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The vehicle routing problems with real-world constraints (we consider vehicles capacity limits, time windows constrains, pickup-and-delivery multi-depo --- CPDPTW) pose significant computational challenges. While classical exact and heuristic methods remain effective to solve problems of small/medium size ($N\lesssim100$), they often lack adaptability and scalability for larger logistics tasks. In this work, we show how JAMPR+/L2D RL deep learning model, proposed in to solve large CPDPTW problems can be adopted in the case of substantial changes of graph distance matrix. We test performance of JAMPR+/L2D model for medium-sized CVRP and VRPTW problems on CVRPLIB benchmarks: JAMPR+/L2D outperforms the state-of-the-art heuristic HGS in over 85\% of instances, achieving improvement in objective gap. We show that the JAMPR+/L2D model trained on CPDPTW problem, generalizes well for tasks with simpler constraints (CVRP, VRPTW), for different problem sizes and for moderate changes in distance matrixes. For more substantial changes in distance matrixes, we propose here to make fast finetuning of JAMPR+: on ORTEC data (for CPDPTW) the proposed strategy remarkably reduces the objective gap without full model retraining, what will give both accuracy and rapid inference of the model in the practical routing scenarios with distance matrix changes.

</details>


### [406] [On the Effect of Bit-Level Parameter Perturbations in Machine Learning and Deep Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26280)
*Akanksha Raghapur,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究经典机器学习模型（HMM、SVM）与深度学习模型（MLP、LSTM）对参数微小定向修改的响应差异，并比较两者的隐写容量。基于Drebin安卓恶意软件数据集，发现经典模型参数脆弱、隐写容量有限，而神经网络参数冗余、隐写容量更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同类别学习模型在参数敏感性和隐写容量上的差异，为理解模型鲁棒性以及隐藏信息嵌入提供统一框架。

Method: 对HMM与SVM等经典模型，以及MLP与LSTM等神经网络，在Drebin安卓恶意软件数据集上进行两组实验：一是对选定参数施加小规模定向修改以观察模型行为变化；二是测量隐写容量，即在不显著损害性能的前提下可被覆盖的参数字节比例。

Result: 经典模型表现出脆弱性，少量选定参数即可显著改变模型行为；其参数结构紧凑、高效且相对敏感，因此隐写容量有限。相比之下，神经网络参数冗余，修改可分散到众多参数中，对性能影响极小，因而具有更高的隐写容量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了经典模型与神经模型在参数变化响应上的本质差异，对模型鲁棒性和隐藏信息嵌入均有明确启示，并提供了一个跨模型类别理解参数敏感性与隐写容量的分析框架。

Abstract: In this chapter, we investigate how classical machine learning models respond to small, targeted modifications in their parameters. We compare and contrast these results to analogous experiments on deep learning models. For classical learning models, we consider Hidden Markov Models (HMM) and Support Vector Machines (SVM), and for comparison, we conduct analogous experiments involving Multilayer Perceptrons (MLP) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks. When applied to the Drebin Android malware dataset, our results show that classical models are brittle, in the sense that a limited set of selected parameters can have a dramatic effect on model behavior. In a related set of experiments, we investigate the steganographic capacity of these same learning models, that is, the proportion of bits in model parameters that can be overwritten without having a significant adverse affect on a model. We find that classical models offer limited steganographic capacity due to their compact, parameter-efficient, and relatively sensitive parameter structure. In contrast, neural networks are parameter-redundant, enabling higher steganographic capacity, where modifications can be distributed across many parameters with minimal impact on performance. These results highlight differences in how classical and neural models respond to parameter changes, with clear implications for both robustness and hidden information embedding. Overall, this work provides a framework for understanding parameter sensitivity and steganographic capacity across different classes of learning models.

</details>


### [407] [Quantifying Protocol-Induced Uncertainty in Comparative Predictive-Model Evaluation: Evidence from Large-Scale Daily PM10 Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26288)
*Rafael da Silva,Kiersten Monahan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"协议诱导的排序不确定性"框架，用协议敏感性评分（PSS）量化切换评估协议对模型排名的影响，并在欧洲425个站点与美国365个监测点的PM10预测研究中验证：协议间排名位移远大于协议内扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较预测模型的研究常以排名收尾，但这些排名依赖于评估协议，而协议对排名的影响很少被视为不确定性来源，可能导致模型选择结论不稳定。

Method: 形式化"协议诱导的排序不确定性"，构建对比框架：比较切换协议造成的排名位移与固定协议下常规选择（协议内扰动）造成的位移；用协议敏感性评分（PSS）和完全重拟合的协议内参照进行量化。在每日PM10大规模序贯预测研究中验证，对比静态划分与滚动原点评估，覆盖欧洲425个背景站和365个美国EPA监测点。

Result: 切换协议的平均PSS为0.801和0.772，分别有35.3%和31.5%的站点改变所选模型；欧洲协议内扰动PSS仅0.072和0.230，胜者交换率0.8%和4.9%，协议间位移显著更大。候选模型从3个增至9个，欧洲协议间胜者交换率升至60.2%，且在冻结协议下对留出站点仍成立。

Conclusion: 模型选择结论会实质性地依赖合理的评估选择。建议在宣称模型优越性的同时，报告在一小组可辩护的协议内扰动下的排名稳定性。

Abstract: Comparative studies of predictive models often end by ranking candidate models, yet these rankings depend on evaluation protocols whose influence is rarely treated as a source of uncertainty. We formalize this problem as protocol-induced ranking uncertainty and introduce a framework that compares ranking displacement caused by switching protocols with displacement produced by conventional choices within a fixed protocol. We quantify these effects using the Protocol Sensitivity Score (PSS) and a full-refit intraprotocol reference.
  We validate the framework in a large-scale sequential prediction study of daily PM10. Static-split and rolling-origin evaluation are compared across 425 European background stations and 365 US EPA monitors. Switching protocols produces mean PSS values of 0.801 and 0.772 and changes the selected model at 35.3% and 31.5% of stations, respectively. In Europe, intraprotocol perturbations with identical scored targets produce PSS values of 0.072 and 0.230, with winner-swap rates of 0.8% and 4.9%. Between-protocol displacement is therefore substantially larger than the selected within-protocol references. Expanding the candidate set from three to nine models increases the between-protocol winner-swap rate to 60.2% in Europe. The pattern also persists under a frozen protocol applied to held-out background and non-background stations. These results show that model-selection conclusions can depend materially on legitimate evaluation choices. We recommend reporting ranking stability under a small set of defensible intraprotocol perturbations alongside claims of model superiority.

</details>


### [408] [CompKV: Compensation-Aware KV Selection for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26300)
*Zhen Huang,Ruizhe Yao,Danyi Liu,Xinrui Chen,Shuwei Li,Siru Zhong,Zijian Cao,Yushan Lai,Mingming Guo,Weijie Zheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CompKV——首个"补偿感知"的稀疏注意力框架，将token分块并针对下游补偿机制优化选择准则，在RULER与LongBench-Pro上表现最优，最高获得6.85倍自注意力加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型长上下文推理受限于KV缓存的内存带宽瓶颈。稀疏注意力虽能通过只对部分token计算精确注意力来加速，但为弥补被排除token的贡献，需引入补偿机制。现有方法先按注意力质量选token、再对未选token做粗粒度补偿，这种"解耦设计"忽略了选择与补偿之间的相互影响——应优先选取"若被忽略会造成最大补偿误差"的token。

Method: 提出CompKV框架：1）将token划分为块；2）显式地针对下游补偿机制优化选择。理论分析表明，块级均值补偿留下的残差由块注意力质量与块内logit变化共同决定；3）用紧凑的块级统计量近似该残差，得到可部署的选择准则；4）设计高效的异步实现。

Result: 在RULER与LongBench-Pro上，CompKV在所评估的稀疏基线中表现最优，相比全注意力最高实现6.85倍的自注意力加速。

Conclusion: 将token选择与补偿机制统一优化（补偿感知）能有效降低补偿误差，兼顾精度与效率，为长上下文LLM推理提供了一种更优的稀疏注意力方案。

Abstract: Despite their strong performance, large language models (LLMs) are bottlenecked by KV cache memory traffic during long-context inference. Sparse attention is widely used to accelerate LLM inference by computing exact attention over a selected subset of tokens. To recover the contribution of tokens excluded from exact attention, recent methods apply coarse-grained compensation to the omitted attention tail. However, existing methods typically select tokens based on attention mass and only then compensate for the unselected tokens. This decoupled design overlooks their interaction: selection should prioritize tokens that would leave the largest compensation error if omitted. To address this limitation, we introduce CompKV, the first compensation-aware sparse attention framework that divides tokens into blocks and explicitly optimizes selection for the downstream compensation mechanism. Our theoretical analysis shows that the residual left by block-level mean compensation is governed by both block attention mass and within-block logit variation. We approximate this residual using compact block-level statistics, yielding a deployable selection criterion. We further develop an efficient asynchronous implementation. Experiments on RULER and LongBench-Pro show that CompKV performs best among the evaluated sparse baselines while delivering up to a $6.85\times$ self-attention speedup over full attention.

</details>


### [409] [PreGS: A Parameter-Transfer-Based Multi-Expert Graph Neural Network for Node Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26310)
*Zhicong Cai,Yinglong Zhang,Xiaoying Hong,Xuewen Xia,Xing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出预训练参数迁移的多专家图神经网络框架 PreGS 及其增强版 PreGSv2，在八个公开图数据集上取得有竞争力的节点分类性能，并通过多组分析验证了其有效性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一聚合机制难以捕捉图数据集中多样的结构模式；而独立训练多个结构分支会带来较大开销，且不一定能得到稳定的节点表示。因此需要一种既能融合多样结构信息、又能降低训练开销并保持稳定性的框架。

Method: PreGS 先预训练一个多头图注意力网络（GAT），将其第一层注意力头的线性变换权重迁移到多个 GraphSAGE 专家中；迁移后的专家被冻结，作为互补的结构分支。融合后的原始节点特征、GAT 头表示与 GraphSAGE 专家表示输入 MLP，其输出再与预训练 GAT 的 logits 融合。PreGSv2 在此基础上引入源级加权与结构门控机制，实现自适应多源特征整合。

Result: 在八个公开图数据集上，PreGS 与 PreGSv2 相较代表性图神经网络基线取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 消融、参数迁移、敏感性、聚合器、可视化及训练时间等分析进一步验证了所提框架的有效性与稳定性。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have achieved strong performance in node classification by aggregating information from graph neighborhoods. However, a single aggregation mechanism may be insufficient to capture diverse structural patterns across graph datasets. Moreover, independently training multiple structural branches can introduce substantial overhead without necessarily producing stable node representations. To address these issues, this paper proposes PreGS, a parameter-transfer-based multi-expert graph neural network framework. PreGS first pretrains a multi-head graph attention network (GAT) and transfers the linear transformation weights of its first-layer attention heads to multiple GraphSAGE experts. The transferred experts are frozen and used as complementary structural branches. The fused raw node features, GAT head representations, and GraphSAGE expert representations are fed into a multilayer perceptron (MLP), whose output is further fused with the pretrained GAT logits. Based on PreGS, we further develop PreGSv2, which introduces source-level weighting and a structural gating mechanism for adaptive multi-source feature integration. Experiments on eight public graph datasets show that PreGS and PreGSv2 achieve competitive performance against representative graph neural network baselines. Ablation, parameter-transfer, sensitivity, aggregator, visualization, and training-time analyses further validate the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/LH-Czc/PreGS.

</details>


### [410] [Disaggregated Quantization: Specializing LLM Prefill and Decode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26333)
*Andrei Panferov,Maximilian Kleinegger,Sweta Priyadarshi,Tijmen Blankevoort,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"分离式量化"（DQ），为预填充（prefill）与解码（decode）阶段分别定制计算格式、权重与存储放置，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升精度，并通过SSD流式加载的卸载式分离预填充（ODP）实现显存受限下的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预填充与解码对量化的需求不同：低精度算术加速提示处理，而紧凑权重减少生成时的内存流量。单一量化方案难以同时最优化两个阶段，因此需要针对性地分离处理以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出分离式量化（DQ）：在解码阶段取消激活量化以提升解码密集任务精度；训练独立的"计算原生"预填充权重以加速提示处理；提出卸载式分离预填充（ODP），将预填充权重从SSD流式加载，按提示长度摊销加载开销；并在vLLM中评估分离式服务，通过训练后量化在高达2.8T参数的模型上验证共享权重格式分离。

Result: 在Qwen 3与Gemma 3上，解码阶段取消激活量化可提升解码密集任务精度且不增加推理成本；独立预填充权重相对仅权重量化加速提示处理，并在2-3比特解码下精度持平或更优。Qwen3.8-27B GGUF场景下，训练NVFP4预填充器使1比特精度在MMLU-Pro提升32.5点、MMMU-Pro提升35.3点，且无需修改解码检查点。27B模型的ODP在8K提示长度下于llama.cpp实现1.78倍首token时间加速。

Conclusion: 分离式量化通过为预填充与解码阶段分别定制量化格式与存储策略，可在不增加推理成本的情况下提升精度并加速提示处理；结合SSD流式加载的ODP方案，可在单设备显存受限时仍获得显著的时延收益，且方法具备扩展至超大规模模型的潜力。

Abstract: Prefill and decode reward different approaches to quantization: low-precision arithmetic accelerates prompt processing, while compact weights reduce memory traffic during generation. We propose "disaggregated quantization" (DQ), which specializes computation formats, weights and storage placement to both of these phases. On Qwen 3 and Gemma 3, removing activation quantization specifically on decode improves accuracy on decode-heavy tasks without increasing inference cost. Training separate compute-native prefill weights accelerates prompt processing relative to weight-only inference while matching or exceeding its accuracy at 2-3-bit decode on both decode-heavy and prefill-heavy tasks. With released Qwen3.8-27B GGUF decoders, training an NVFP4 prefiller improves 1-bit accuracy by 32.5 points on MMLU-Pro and 35.3 on MMMU-Pro without modifying the decode checkpoint. To accommodate the additional checkpoint on a single device, offloaded disaggregated prefill (ODP) streams its weights from SSD, amortizing loading over prompt length. On the same 27B model, ODP delivers a 1.78x time-to-first-token speedup over the weight-only baseline at 8K prompt length in llama.cpp. We evaluate accuracy under disaggregated serving in vLLM and further validate shared-weight format disaggregation through post-training quantization on models up to 2.8T parameters.

</details>


### [411] [Geometry-Aware Hyperbolic Residual Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26342)
*Alessio Colombo,Melika Ayoughi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出几何感知的双曲残差量化方法，在前向与反向传播中分别解决双曲加法非结合性与直通估计忽略几何的问题，改善层级化离散潜在空间的组织稳定性，但纯压缩任务仍以欧氏方法为优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差向量量化将连续表示转为多层级离散token，其码本天然具有由粗到细的层级结构，但现有方法多在欧氏空间进行，忽略了数据中的潜在层级。双曲几何适合建模层级，然而直接将残差量化扩展到双曲空间会带来几何不一致：双曲加法非结合导致残差聚合不一致，标准直通梯度估计也忽视潜在空间几何。

Method: 提出几何感知的双曲残差量化，同时修正前向与反向传播。前向中采用双曲残差聚合（Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation），在Poincaré球上恢复残差量化的伸缩（telescoping）行为；反向中采用带折扣的双曲直通估计器（Discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator），将重建梯度作为单一几何块回传，避免跨残差阶段的不稳定递归梯度传递。

Result: 在层级预测、推荐、图像token化和神经音频编码任务上，相比朴素双曲基线，本方法提升了双曲残差码的稳定性与结构组织性。同时观察到明显的结构—压缩权衡：纯压缩场景下欧氏残差量化仍更优，而几何感知双曲量化更适合具有层级组织的离散潜在空间。

Conclusion: 双曲残差量化的关键挑战在于前向聚合与反向梯度的几何一致性；通过几何感知的前向聚合与折扣直通估计可有效缓解。方法的价值在于层级化离散潜在空间的结构组织而非压缩效率，实际应用中应根据任务性质在欧氏与双曲量化间取舍。

Abstract: Residual Vector Quantization turns continuous representations into discrete, multi-level token sequences. Yet most methods operate in Euclidean space, despite the coarse-to-fine structure of the resulting codes and the latent hierarchies present in many data domains. Hyperbolic geometry offers a natural alternative for hierarchical representations, but naive hyperbolic extensions introduce geometric inconsistencies: non-associative hyperbolic addition prevents consistent residual aggregation, while standard straight-through gradient estimation ignores the geometry of the latent space. We propose a geometry-aware hyperbolic residual quantization that addresses these issues in both the forward and backward passes. In the forward pass, Hyperbolic Residual Aggregation restores the telescoping behavior of residual quantization on the Poincare ball. In the backward pass, a discounted Hyperbolic Straight-Through Estimator routes the reconstruction gradient through the quantizer as a single geometric block, avoiding unstable recursive gradient transport across residual stages. Evaluations on hierarchical prediction, recommendation, image tokenization, and neural audio coding tasks show that our method improves the stability and structural organization of hyperbolic residual codes over naive hyperbolic baselines. At the same time, we observe a clear structure-compression trade-off: Euclidean residual quantization remains preferable for pure compression, while geometry-aware hyperbolic quantization is most useful for hierarchically organized discrete latent spaces.

</details>


### [412] [PACT: From Credit Assignment to Critic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26355)
*Jiayan Fu,Hang Xu,Yong Zhang,Zhaokai Luo,Yao Hu,Dongyan Zhao,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出token级信用（token-level credit）的三个正则性条件（完备性、前缀一致性、中立性），证明其可唯一确定token级信用，并基于此提出PACT方法，在数学推理和SWE-bench上显著优于GRPO、PPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为大语言模型后训练的核心环节，但token级信用缺乏公认的数学定义，导致其与常用训练信号（如RLOO、OPD、GAE）之间的关系不清晰，需要建立统一的数学刻画来指导算法设计与分析。

Method: 作者提出完备性、前缀一致性、中立性三个正则性条件，证明它们唯一确定token级信用；用该框架解释OPD中的理想教师等价于隐式critic、RLOO信号与token级信用的期望策略梯度贡献一致，并证明有界结果奖励下的近似信用稀疏性及GAE中间critic误差问题；进而提出PACT（Policy Aligned Critic Training），采用Actor-then-Critic更新顺序，对critic训练施加重要性采样校正以更好对齐更新后的策略。

Result: 在agentic数学推理的四个基准上，PACT平均准确率达72.87%，分别比GRPO和PPO高8.80和13.16个百分点；在SWE-bench Verified上通过率达67.4%，分别比PPO、GRPO和SAO高2.4、2.0和3.8个百分点。

Conclusion: token级信用可被三个正则性条件唯一刻画，这一统一视角既解释了现有算法现象，也指导了PACT的设计。PACT通过Actor-then-Critic顺序和重要性采样校正改善了critic与策略的对齐，在数学推理和代码任务上均取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central component of large language model (LLM) post-training, yet token-level credit lacks a generally accepted mathematical definition, leaving its relationship to commonly used training signals unclear. We formulate three regularity conditions, namely Completeness, Prefix Consistency, and Neutrality, and prove that they uniquely determine token-level credit. This characterization provides a unified basis for explaining phenomena across existing algorithms and guides the development of an improved actor-critic training procedure. Through this lens, an ideal teacher in On-Policy Distillation (OPD) acts as an implicit critic, yielding an expected policy gradient proportional to that induced by token-level credit. Response-level REINFORCE Leave-One-Out (RLOO) signals match the expected policy-gradient contribution of token-level credit despite their coarser granularity. We further establish approximate credit sparsity under bounded outcome rewards and show how intermediate critic errors in Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) can become comparable to the underlying credit. These motivate Policy Aligned Critic Training (PACT), which adopts an Actor-then-Critic update order to apply importance sampling correction to critic training and better align the critic with the updated policy. In agentic mathematical reasoning, PACT achieves 72.87% average accuracy across four benchmarks, outperforming GRPO and PPO by 8.80 and 13.16 percentage points, respectively. On SWE-bench Verified, PACT achieves a pass rate of 67.4%, outperforming PPO, GRPO, and SAO by 2.4, 2.0, and 3.8 percentage points, respectively.

</details>


### [413] [FairMean: Promoting Fairness in Distributed Learning under Label Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26377)
*Huigan Zheng,Jiaojiao Zhang,Yongxiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FairMean方法，通过有界非递减函数对客户端梯度加权，在标签投毒场景下缓解公平性与鲁棒性之间的冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平感知的分布式学习会优先照顾损失大的客户端以缩小性能差距，但标签投毒会人为制造大损失，从而诱发公平性与鲁棒性之间的冲突。

Method: FairMean使用基于本地损失的有界非递减函数对客户端梯度进行加权：递增的权重偏向高损失客户端以促进公平，而上界则限制因损失引起的对中毒客户端梯度的过度放大。

Result: 在无标签投毒时，最小化FairMean目标比最小化标准平均损失目标更有利于解的公平性；在标签投毒下，建立了平均稳定点界，其中依赖攻击的项与中毒客户端比例的平方成正比。实验表明FairMean通过降低准确率方差促进公平，同时提升最差客户端准确率。

Conclusion: FairMean能够有效管理公平性与鲁棒性之间的冲突，在提升公平性的同时也改善了最差客户端表现。

Abstract: Fairness-aware distributed learning prioritizes clients with large losses to reduce performance disparities, but label poisoning can create large losses, thereby inducing a fairness--robustness conflict. We propose FairMean to manage this conflict. FairMean weights client gradients using a bounded, nondecreasing function of local loss. The increasing weights prioritize high-loss clients to promote fairness, while the upper bound prevents excessive loss-induced amplification of poisoned-client gradients. In the absence of label poisoning, we show that minimizing the FairMean objective is more conducive to solution fairness than minimizing the standard average-loss objective. Under label poisoning, we establish an average-stationarity bound whose attack-dependent term is proportional to the square of the poisoned-client fraction. Experiments show that FairMean promotes fairness by reducing accuracy variance while improving worst-client accuracy.

</details>


### [414] [Learning to Defer with Guidance on Real World Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26384)
*Emma Sun,Joshua Strong,Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在真实医学影像数据（Collab-CXR、VinDr-CXR、CheXpert）上验证两阶段 Learning to Defer（L2D），并提出带指导的 L2D（L2D with Guidance），将决策空间扩展为自主预测、转交人类专家、转交并提供 AI 指导三种选择，实验表明其性能优于经典 L2D 及各类基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像解读工作量大且耗时，AI 可减轻负担，但完全自主部署存在安全隐患，低特异性可能反而增加临床医生工作负荷。L2D 理论上已有保证，但尚未在带有人类标注的真实医学数据集上验证其性能。

Method: 评估两阶段 L2D 的 predictor-rejector 形式（AI 预测模型固定，路由/拒绝器可训练），在带多人类标注的多标签胸片数据集 Collab-CXR 上进行；提出 L2D with Guidance，将决策空间扩展为三种选择：自主预测、转交人类专家、转交人类专家并提供 AI 指导；比较多种拒绝器架构、损失函数和不同输入特征可用性，并在 VinDr-CXR 和 CheXpert 上复现。

Result: 带有 Guidance 的两阶段 L2D 优于经典两阶段 L2D，以及人类单独、AI 单独和 AI 指导人类的基线。值得注意的是，这一性能是在使用比现有文献中正式定义的 L2D 代理损失更简单的损失函数下实现的。

Conclusion: 这是首个在带人类标注的真实医学影像数据背景下研究 L2D 的工作。结果表明，引入 AI 指导的三分支决策路由可提升医学影像人机协作性能，且无需复杂的代理损失函数，为 L2D 在实际临床场景中的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Medical image interpretation is high-volume and time-consuming, and while AI interpretation can reduce workload, fully autonomous deployment carries potential safety concerns and low specificity may in practice lead to increased clinician workload. Learning to Defer (L2D) addresses this by selectively routing cases between autonomous prediction and human experts by learning from input features and AI model and human performance. While theoretical guarantees have been proven for L2D, its performance has not been validated on real-world medical datasets with human reader annotations. We evaluate the predictor-rejector formulation of two-stage L2D, where the AI predictor model is fixed and separate from the trainable routing or rejector model, on Collab-CXR, a multilabel chest X-ray dataset with multiple human annotations per case. This is the first work to look at L2D in the context of real-world medical imaging data with human annotations. We further introduce a new setup, L2D with Guidance, where the decision space is extended to three choices: predict autonomously, defer to a human expert, or defer to a human expert and provide AI guidance. We compare multiple rejector architectures and loss functions, and different input feature availabilities. This is reproduced on two larger datasets, VinDr-CXR and CheXpert. Our results show that two-stage L2D with Guidance outperforms classic two-stage learning to defer, as well as human-alone, AI-alone and AI-guided human baselines. Notably, this performance is achieved with simpler loss functions compared to formally defined L2D surrogate loss functions in current literature.

</details>


### [415] [TimeInteract: Towards Real-Time Interactive Intelligence for Streaming Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26389)
*Sheng Pan,Yongli Gu,Yiqing Guo,Warren Jin,Bo Du,Shirui Pan,Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"时间序列交互"新范式，并开发TimeInteract模型，让模型在持续接收流式时序观测的同时自主决定何时响应，且响应生成期间不阻塞后续观测处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的时间序列是连续演化的，有意义的变化可能在任何时刻出现。然而现有时序语言模型（TSLM）本质上是静态的：要么接收完整序列做离线处理，要么在流式输入与响应生成之间交替，导致在交互过程中无法处理新到达的观测。因此需要一个能持续感知并自主决定何时响应、且生成响应时仍能继续处理新数据的新范式。

Method: 提出TimeInteract，包含三项关键设计：(1) 双视角流式时序编码器，捕捉局部变化与历史动态；(2) 响应控制机制，学习何时触发响应；(3) 解耦的流式推理机制，将控制与响应生成分离，避免阻塞后续观测。此外，构建了从"理解"到"自适应"的交互能力层级，并基于此构建大规模流式时序交互数据集StreamTSI-34K（34,588个片段、77,505条响应，涵盖合成与真实时序、单轮与多轮设定）。

Result: 在全部四个交互层级上，TimeInteract持续优于现有LLM、VLM和TSLM，在挑战性任务上提升最高达23.92分；同时改进了响应触发，实现近乎零的流停顿，推理速度最高提升2.15倍。

Conclusion: TimeInteract验证了流式时序交互范式的有效性，在理解与自适应能力上全面领先，并兼顾了触发准确性、流处理连续性与推理效率，为时序语言模型从静态走向连续交互提供了新方向。

Abstract: Real-world time series evolve continuously, with meaningful changes potentially emerging at any moment. However, existing time-series language models (TSLMs) remain inherently static. They either receive complete sequences for offline processing or alternate between streaming input and response generation, which prevents processing of new observations during interaction. We introduce a new regime, Time-Series Interaction: a model continuously perceives incoming time-series observations and user intent, autonomously decides when to remain silent or respond, and continues processing new observations during response generation. To realize this, we develop TimeInteract with three key designs: a dual-view streaming TS encoder that captures local variations and historical dynamics, a response control mechanism that learns when to trigger a response, and a decoupled streaming inference mechanism that separates control from response generation to avoid blocking subsequent observations. We further formulate a hierarchy of interaction capabilities, progressing from Understanding to Adaptivity. Based on this hierarchy, we construct StreamTSI-34K, a large-scale streaming TS interaction dataset with 34,588 episodes and 77,505 responses across synthetic and real-world time series in single- and multi-turn settings. Across all four interaction levels, TimeInteract consistently outperforms existing LLMs, VLMs, and TSLMs, with gains of up to 23.92 points on challenging tasks. It also improves response triggering while achieving near-zero stream stall and up to $2.15\times$ inference speedup.

</details>


### [416] [Double Descent and Malign Overfitting in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26392)
*Raphaël Urfin,Tony Bonnaire,Giulio Biroli,Marc Mézard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 扩散模型的过拟合是恶性的而非良性的：测试损失在 p~n 处开始上升（远早于 p~nm 的插值峰值），因隐式正则化将模型推向记忆训练集的"经验得分"而非真实得分；但配合最优正则化后，过参数化仍有益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中普遍认为过参数化是良性的（插值模型泛化好、呈双下降曲线），而扩散模型训练本质上是最小化二次得分匹配损失的回归问题，理应同样良性。但实际观察到扩散模型的过拟合是灾难性的，会进入记忆模式。本文旨在解决这一悖论。

Method: 将 CelebA 上训练的 U-Net 实验与随机特征模型结合，后者可推导出闭式学习曲线；引入每个训练样本固定数量 m 个噪声实现；通过偏差-方差分解定位机制。

Result: 插值峰值出现在 p~nm 处而非标准回归的 p~n；但测试损失上升在 p~n 处就开始，与 m 无关。偏差在 p~n 处开始增长，峰值后方差衰减而偏差持续增长并饱和于较大值。由于扩散模型用 m≫1 训练，峰值被推到极大模型尺寸，实际处于上升分支上，恶性过拟合已然发生。

Conclusion: 过拟合恶性是因为隐式正则化虽在起作用，却把模型推向记忆训练集的经验得分而非真实得分。但当配合正则化（岭惩罚或早停）时，过参数化仍有益：最优正则化的大模型优于任何未正则化模型。

Abstract: Conventional wisdom in deep learning holds that overparameterization---having more parameters $p$ than training samples $n$---is benign: larger models generalize better and, even without regularization, interpolating models generalize well, the test error following a double-descent curve. One might expect the same benign overfitting for diffusion models, whose training reduces to regression, i.e. to minimizing a quadratic score-matching loss. Yet the opposite is observed: overfitting here is catastrophic, driving the model into a memorization regime. We resolve this paradox by combining experiments on U-Nets trained on CelebA with a random-features model for which we derive closed-form learning curves. We show that with a fixed number $m$ of noise realizations per training sample, an interpolation peak does occur, but at $p\sim nm$ rather than at $p\sim n$ as in standard regression. The rise of the test loss, however, sets in much earlier, at $p\sim n$, independently of $m$. This overfitting is malign because, although the implicit regularization of training is fully at work, it drives the model toward the empirical score, which memorizes the training set, rather than toward the true score. A bias-variance decomposition pinpoints the mechanism: the bias of the score estimator starts to grow at $p\sim n$; past the peak the variance decays, as in regression, whereas the bias keeps growing and both saturate at a large value. Since diffusion models are trained with $m\gg1$, the peak is pushed to very large model sizes, and therefore sit on the rising branch that precedes it, where malign overfitting is already in play. Nevertheless, overparameterization remains beneficial when paired with regularization: in the random-features theory and in U-Net experiments, optimally regularized large models---via a ridge penalty or early stopping, respectively---outperform any unregularized models.

</details>


### [417] [OMatG-flash: An All-Atom Flow Map with Reinforce Adjoint Matching for Scalable Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26402)
*Thomas Egg,Harry Winston Sullivan,Ellad B. Tadmor,Stefano Martiniani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 OMatG-flash——一种全原子流映射模型，用于无机晶体结构预测与从头生成，以更少推理步数和时间达到 SOTA 水平，是材料生成的帕累托最优推理引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型无机材料的发现对计算、储能等领域至关重要。生成式 AI 有望加速材料发现流程，但当前主流的流模型与扩散模型受制于候选材料提议的高昂成本（推理步数多、耗时长）。

Method: 提出 OMatG-flash，一种面向无机晶体结构预测（CSP）与从头生成（DNG）的全原子流映射（all-atom flow map）。并引入 Reinforce Adjoint Matching 对流映射进行后训练微调。

Result: 相比现有流模型与扩散模型，推理步数减少一个数量级，墙钟时间更短，同时基准性能与 SOTA 相当；在无条件 CSP 任务上进一步提升了匹配率与 RMSE。

Conclusion: OMatG-flash 展示了流映射在加速生成高质量候选无机材料方面的潜力，并在满足数据密集型材料发现工作流所需样本吞吐量方面迈进一步。

Abstract: The discovery of novel inorganic materials drives technological breakthroughs in critical fields such as computing and energy storage. Generative AI has promised to accelerate the materials discovery pipeline, but state-of-the-art flow and diffusion models remain bottlenecked by the cost of proposing candidate materials. To address this, we introduce OMatG-flash, an all-atom flow map for inorganic crystal structure prediction (CSP) and de novo generation (DNG). OMatG-flash is a Pareto-optimal inference engine for materials, sampling candidate materials with an order of magnitude fewer inference steps and less wall-clock time than existing flow and diffusion models while demonstrating benchmark performance on par with the state-of-the-art. To enable post-training fine-tuning we apply Reinforce Adjoint Matching to flow maps, further improving match rates and RMSE on the unconditional CSP task. OMatG-flash showcases the potential of flow maps to accelerate generation of high-quality candidate inorganic materials and demonstrates a step forward in sample throughput necessary for data-hungry materials discovery workflows.

</details>


### [418] [DeepFEAv2: Deep Learning for Transient Finite Element Analysis Beyond Structured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26426)
*Georgios Triantafyllou,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepFEAv2，一个可跨不同有限元网格拓扑与单元类型进行瞬态FEA预测的深度学习代理框架，在结构化与非结构化3D数据集及主动脉瓣数据集上取得高精度并大幅提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）计算成本高，限制实时与高分辨率应用；现有深度学习代理模型多局限于稳态预测或无法联合预测节点与单元输出（NEO），且最先进的DeepFEA仅适用于结构化网格。

Method: 提出DeepFEAv2，包含三个模块：(a) 利用FE连接矩阵按单元组织输入特征，并由网格拓扑引导排成输入序列；(b) 新神经网络架构处理该序列并联合预测随时间变化的NEO；(c) 基于FEA信息的优化策略以正则化NEO预测。

Result: 在结构化与非结构化3D线弹性数据集及压力驱动主动脉瓣数据集上评估，R^2最高达0.99，归一化误差最低0.38%；相较DeepFEA，R^2相对提升最高38.0%，归一化误差降低最高87.1%；推理速度比传统FEA快最多三个数量级。

Conclusion: DeepFEAv2能高效建模日益复杂的有限元设置下的瞬态FEA仿真，为瞬态FEA提供了可扩展的代理框架。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) is widely used for transient mechanical simulations, but its high computational cost limits real-time and high-resolution applications. Deep learning surrogate models can reduce this cost; however, many existing approaches are restricted to steady-state prediction or cannot jointly predict Node- and Element-based Outputs (NEO) over time. The state-of-the-art DeepFEA framework has addressed these issues but remains limited to structured finite element (FE) meshes. To overcome this limitation, this study proposes DeepFEAv2, a deep learning surrogate framework that enables prediction of transient FEA simulations across different FE mesh topologies and element types. The main contributions of DeepFEAv2 are: (a) a module that uses the FE connectivity matrix to organize input features by element and arrange them into an input sequence guided by the mesh topology; (b) a novel neural network architecture designed to process the input sequence and jointly predict NEO over time; and (c) a FEA-informed optimization strategy for regularizing these NEO predictions. DeepFEAv2 was evaluated on structured and unstructured 3D linear elastic datasets, as well as on a pressure-driven aortic valve dataset. DeepFEAv2 achieved R^2 values up to 0.99 and normalized errors as low as 0.38%. Compared with DeepFEA, it achieved up to 38.0% relative increase in R^2 and up to 87.1% reduction in normalized error. DeepFEAv2 also performed inference up to three orders of magnitude faster than traditional FEA. These results demonstrate that DeepFEAv2 can efficiently model transient FEA simulations across increasingly complex FE settings, providing a scalable surrogate framework for transient FEA.

</details>


### [419] [One-Step Generative Surrogate Models via Block-Triangular Joint Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26435)
*Nicholas Geissler,Shreya Jha,Ricardo Baptista,Benjamin Peherstorfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"块三角联合漂移"方法，利用投影漂移场作用于相邻状态的联合分布，实现单步随机转移建模，每次时间步仅需一次模型评估即可生成随机轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漂移（Drifting）能直接构建单步生成模型，但直接用于随机转移建模时，需要对同一当前状态采样多个下一状态样本。然而标准轨迹数据对每个当前状态通常只提供一个实际观测到的下一状态，无法对下一状态的条件分布进行经验近似。

Method: 提出块三角联合漂移：将投影漂移场应用于经验可获取的相邻状态联合分布上。块三角结构保持当前状态的边缘分布不变，同时使第二分量成为下一状态条件分布的直接采样器。所得代理模型每个时间步只需一次模型评估，无需步骤间的辅助生成过程。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够准确复现边缘统计量和轨迹相关统计量，并且在精度–成本权衡方面优于确定性、扩散、流以及蒸馏类生成代理模型。

Conclusion: 块三角联合漂移为从单样本轨迹数据中学习单步随机转移模型提供了可行方案，兼顾采样效率与统计准确性。

Abstract: Drifting provides a direct route to one-step generative models, but applying it directly to stochastic transition modeling requires multiple samples of the next state conditioned on the same current state. Standard trajectory data, however, typically provide only one realized next state for each observed current state and therefore do not provide an empirical approximation of the corresponding conditional distribution over possible next states. We introduce block-triangular joint drifting, which instead applies a projected drift field to the empirically accessible joint distribution of consecutive states. Importantly, the block-triangular architecture preserves the current-state marginal while making its second component a direct sampler of the conditional distribution of possible next states. The resulting surrogate generates stochastic trajectories with one model evaluation per time step, without auxiliary generative steps between time steps. Numerical experiments demonstrate accurate marginal and trajectory-dependent statistics and favorable accuracy-cost tradeoffs compared with deterministic, diffusion-, flow-, and distillation-based generative surrogate models.

</details>


### [420] [Can We Predict Anomaly Detection Performance from Embedding-Space Geometry?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26460)
*Kevin Wilkinghoff,Zheng-Hua Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究无异常数据条件下的异常检测性能预测与模型选择，提出基于伪异常探针的估计方法，在DCASE基准上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测系统通常仅用正常数据训练，但模型选择与评估依赖标注异常。作者希望探究在无异常数据情况下能否预测检测性能，解决无异常模型选择难题。

Method: 针对kNN检测器，推导AUC下界，将检测性能与内点/离群点分数分离度及其方差关联；在局部缩放模型下分析密度变化、内在维度异质性与跨域失配对分数变异的影响；引入简单伪异常探针，为估计相对分数分离度提供参考。

Result: 在DCASE 2022-2025基准上，涵盖4种嵌入模型与208个候选系统，基于伪异常的估计器显著提升无异常模型选择效果；多样性伪异常使无异常模型选择在域偏移下优于传统开发集选择。

Conclusion: 嵌入空间几何包含关于异常检测性能的预测信息，同时揭示仅用内点估计性能具有表征依赖性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection systems are often trained using normal data alone, while model selection and evaluation typically require labeled anomalies. We study whether anomaly detection performance can be predicted without access to anomalous data. For kNN-based detectors, we derive a lower bound on the area under the ROC curve (AUC) that relates detection performance to the separation between inlier and outlier scores and to their respective variances. Under a local scaling model, we use this bound to characterize how density variation, intrinsic-dimensional heterogeneity, and cross-domain mismatch contribute to score variability. We then investigate anomaly-free model selection and show that inlier score variance alone does not reliably predict performance across different representations. To address this limitation, we introduce simple pseudo-anomaly probes that provide a reference for estimating relative score separation. Experiments on the DCASE 2022-2025 benchmarks, spanning four embedding models and 208 candidate systems, show that pseudo-anomaly-based estimators substantially improve anomaly-free model selection. In particular, diverse pseudo-anomalies enable anomaly-free model selection to outperform conventional development-set selection under domain shift. These results show that embedding-space geometry contains predictive information about anomaly detection performance while also highlighting the representation-dependent nature of inlier-only performance estimates.

</details>


### [421] [When Recursive Models Finish Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26487)
*Hare Krishna,Shubham Singh,Stephen Ebert,Hao-Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 递归模型可在名义推理预算之外继续更新隐状态，本文通过将Tiny Recursive Models的递归步数从16扩展到512，研究其在困难数独上的完成动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在名义推理预算下输出错误，无法判断是计算尚未完成还是进入了持续失败的机制，因此需要研究递归模型完成计算的动力学特征。

Method: 在1000个困难数独上，对基于注意力和基于MLP的Tiny Recursive Models，将递归从名义16步扩展到512步，并分析隐状态运动、局部雅可比稳定性及扰动实验。

Result: 扩展递归后，注意力模型累计精确求解准确率从59.2%升至87.5%，MLP模型从74.4%升至91.9%，解决超过三分之二名义预算下未解的谜题；首次精确解后隐状态运动急剧下降，完成状态沿轨迹方向局部收缩但雅可比仍含强扩张方向。

Conclusion: 结果区分了名义预算失败与计算完成，并识别出两种递归架构共有的完成动力学特征，即轨迹条件下的各向异性稳定性。

Abstract: Recursive models can continue updating their latent states beyond their nominal inference budget, so an incorrect output at that budget does not show whether computation is unfinished or has entered a persistently unsuccessful regime. We study the dynamics of completion in attention- and MLP-based Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) on 1,000 hard Sudoku puzzles. Extending recurrence from the nominal 16 steps to 512 steps increases cumulative exact-solve accuracy from 59.2% to 87.5% for the attention model and from 74.4% to 91.9% for the MLP model, solving more than two-thirds of the puzzles unsolved in the nominal budget. Across both architectures, latent-state motion drops sharply after the first exact solution. Completed states are typically locally contractive along the trajectory direction, even though the same local Jacobian retains strongly expanding directions. We characterize this phenomenon as trajectory-conditioned anisotropic stability. Perturbation experiments confirm this directional stability across both models. The multi-step fate of the maximally expanding direction differs: it is absorbed within 16 steps in the attention model but persists longer in the MLP model. The anisotropic-stability pattern also holds for a second attention checkpoint. Together, these results distinguish nominal-budget failure from completed computation and identify a common dynamical signature of completion across two recurrent architectures.

</details>


### [422] [Gap-Free Streaming PCA Beyond Rank-One Updates: Near-Optimal Rates and Applications to Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26508)
*Anming Gu,Syamantak Kumar,Kevin Tian,Chutong Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对无间隙流式 PCA 问题给出了 Oja 算法的新分析，并在仅依赖二阶矩界条件下得到近乎匹配的上下界，进而提供无间隙子高斯数据的差分隐私 PCA 保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式 PCA 旨在单遍扫描数据流时恢复主谱子空间。已有近最优速率的结果要么依赖特征间隙假设，要么局限于秩一更新，因此需要一种更通用的无间隙分析，并解决相关开放问题。

Method: 对 Oja 算法进行新的理论分析，覆盖最一般的无间隙变体，不假设底层均值矩阵存在特征间隙。证明仅使用单次随机更新的二阶矩界，避免了先前近优分析所需的几乎必然界以及离线矩阵 Bernstein 界。并将结果扩展到 Rayleigh 商意义下的近似 PCA。

Result: 得到流式 PCA 的近乎匹配的下界与上界；证明在更弱假设下成立；扩展至 Rayleigh 商近似 PCA；作为主要应用，为子高斯数据给出无间隙差分隐私 PCA 保证，在忽略对数因子的意义下解决了 [Bro26] 的猜想 1.1。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推进了无间隙流式 PCA 的理论分析，降低了所需概率假设，回应了 [JJK+16] 的开放问题，并取得差分隐私 PCA 的重要应用。

Abstract: Streaming principal component analysis (PCA) seeks to recover a leading spectral subspace in a single pass over a data stream. We give a new analysis of the ubiquitous Oja's algorithm [Oja82] for the most general, gap-free variant of this problem, where no eigengap assumptions are made on the underlying mean matrix, complemented by a nearly-matching lower bound. Prior works achieving near-optimal rates for streaming PCA either required gap assumptions [JJK+16, HNWW21], or were limited to rank-one updates [AZL17, Lia23]. Our proof only uses a second moment bound on the individual stochastic updates, bypassing the almost sure bounds needed by prior near-optimal analyses, and the analogous offline matrix Bernstein bound. We also extend our result to a Rayleigh quotient notion of approximate PCA, addressing an open question of [JJK+16].
